
## All Links
<!--@include: ./metrics-link-preamble.md-->

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">link_&#8203;frag_&#8203;consumed</span> | counter | Fragments consumed by the link reader |
| <span class="metrics-name">link_&#8203;frag_&#8203;consumed_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes read by the link consumer |
| <span class="metrics-name">link_&#8203;frag_&#8203;filtered</span> | counter | Fragments filtered and not consumed |
| <span class="metrics-name">link_&#8203;frag_&#8203;filtered_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes read by the link consumer that were filtered |
| <span class="metrics-name">link_&#8203;link_&#8203;polling_&#8203;overrun</span> | counter | Times the link was overrun while polling |
| <span class="metrics-name">link_&#8203;frag_&#8203;polling_&#8203;overrun</span> | counter | Fragments not processed because the link was overrun while polling |
| <span class="metrics-name">link_&#8203;link_&#8203;reading_&#8203;overrun</span> | counter | Times the link was overrun while reading metadata |
| <span class="metrics-name">link_&#8203;frag_&#8203;reading_&#8203;overrun</span> | counter | Fragments not processed because the link was overrun while reading |
| <span class="metrics-name">link_&#8203;backpressure_&#8203;blamed</span> | counter | Producer housekeeping intervals in which the producer was backpressured and this reliable consumer had the least credit across its outs; sampled, not an event count |
</div>

## All Tiles
<!--@include: ./metrics-tile-preamble.md-->

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;pid</span> | gauge | Process ID of the tile |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;tid</span> | gauge | Thread ID of the tile. Same as Pid in production, but might differ in development |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;last_&#8203;cpu_&#8203;index</span> | gauge | CPU the tile last ran on |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;context_&#8203;switch_&#8203;involuntary</span> | counter | Involuntary context switches |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;context_&#8203;switch_&#8203;voluntary</span> | counter | Voluntary context switches |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;page_&#8203;fault_&#8203;major</span> | counter | Major page faults |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;page_&#8203;fault_&#8203;minor</span> | counter | Minor page faults |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Tile lifecycle state: 0=booting, 1=running, 2=shutdown |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;heartbeat_&#8203;time</span> | gauge | Last wall-clock time the tile heartbeated |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;is_&#8203;backpressured</span> | gauge | Whether the tile is currently backpressured |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;backpressure</span> | counter | Times the tile waited for one or more consumers to catch up to resume publishing |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{tile_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">caught_&#8203;up_&#8203;housekeeping</span>"} | counter | Tile wall time, by regime; regimes are exhaustive and groups Busy, Idle, Backpressure and Housekeeping partition it (Caught up + Housekeeping) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{tile_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">processing_&#8203;housekeeping</span>"} | counter | Tile wall time, by regime; regimes are exhaustive and groups Busy, Idle, Backpressure and Housekeeping partition it (Processing + Housekeeping) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{tile_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">backpressure_&#8203;housekeeping</span>"} | counter | Tile wall time, by regime; regimes are exhaustive and groups Busy, Idle, Backpressure and Housekeeping partition it (Backpressure + Housekeeping) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{tile_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">caught_&#8203;up_&#8203;prefrag</span>"} | counter | Tile wall time, by regime; regimes are exhaustive and groups Busy, Idle, Backpressure and Housekeeping partition it (Caught up + Prefrag) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{tile_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">processing_&#8203;prefrag</span>"} | counter | Tile wall time, by regime; regimes are exhaustive and groups Busy, Idle, Backpressure and Housekeeping partition it (Processing + Prefrag) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{tile_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">backpressure_&#8203;prefrag</span>"} | counter | Tile wall time, by regime; regimes are exhaustive and groups Busy, Idle, Backpressure and Housekeeping partition it (Backpressure + Prefrag) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{tile_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">caught_&#8203;up_&#8203;postfrag</span>"} | counter | Tile wall time, by regime; regimes are exhaustive and groups Busy, Idle, Backpressure and Housekeeping partition it (Caught up + Postfrag) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{tile_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">processing_&#8203;postfrag</span>"} | counter | Tile wall time, by regime; regimes are exhaustive and groups Busy, Idle, Backpressure and Housekeeping partition it (Processing + Postfrag) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{tile_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">caught_&#8203;up_&#8203;sleeping</span>"} | counter | Tile wall time, by regime; regimes are exhaustive and groups Busy, Idle, Backpressure and Housekeeping partition it (Caught up + Sleeping) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{tile_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">backpressure_&#8203;sleeping</span>"} | counter | Tile wall time, by regime; regimes are exhaustive and groups Busy, Idle, Backpressure and Housekeeping partition it (Backpressure + Sleeping) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;thread_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{thread_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">wait</span>"} | counter | Thread wall time since start, by scheduler state; Idle is the remainder, so parts sum to about the lifetime.  User, System and Interrupt have 10 ms resolution; Interrupt is 0 unless the tile is pinned (Wait (task was runnable but not scheduled)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;thread_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{thread_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">idle</span>"} | counter | Thread wall time since start, by scheduler state; Idle is the remainder, so parts sum to about the lifetime.  User, System and Interrupt have 10 ms resolution; Interrupt is 0 unless the tile is pinned (Idle (task was not runnable)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;thread_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{thread_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">user</span>"} | counter | Thread wall time since start, by scheduler state; Idle is the remainder, so parts sum to about the lifetime.  User, System and Interrupt have 10 ms resolution; Interrupt is 0 unless the tile is pinned (User (task was scheduled and executing in user mode)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;thread_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{thread_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">system</span>"} | counter | Thread wall time since start, by scheduler state; Idle is the remainder, so parts sum to about the lifetime.  User, System and Interrupt have 10 ms resolution; Interrupt is 0 unless the tile is pinned (System (task was scheduled and executing in kernel mode)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;thread_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{thread_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">interrupt</span>"} | counter | Thread wall time since start, by scheduler state; Idle is the remainder, so parts sum to about the lifetime.  User, System and Interrupt have 10 ms resolution; Interrupt is 0 unless the tile is pinned (Interrupt (CPU time stolen by hardirq/softirq handlers or a hypervisor; fixed tiles only)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;park_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{park_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">ring</span>"} | counter | Times the tile tried to park while idle (efficient mode), by result (Woken by a doorbell ring) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;park_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{park_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">deadline</span>"} | counter | Times the tile tried to park while idle (efficient mode), by result (Deadline or park cap lapsed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;park_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{park_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">pending</span>"} | counter | Times the tile tried to park while idle (efficient mode), by result (Pre-park recheck found work, did not park) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;device_&#8203;irq</span> | counter | Device IRQs on the tile's dedicated CPU, 0 for tiles not pinned |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;tlb_&#8203;shootdown</span> | counter | TLB shootdowns on the tile's dedicated CPU, 0 for tiles not pinned |
| <span class="metrics-name">tile_&#8203;timer_&#8203;irq</span> | counter | Local timer interrupts (LOC) on the tile's dedicated CPU, 0 for tiles not pinned |

</div>

## Ipecho Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;shred_&#8203;version</span> | gauge | Shred version used by the validator |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;conn_&#8203;open</span> | gauge | Open connections to the ipecho service |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;conn_&#8203;closed</span><br/>{conn_&#8203;close_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">accepted</span>"} | counter | Connections to the ipecho service made and closed, by outcome (Closed normally) |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;conn_&#8203;closed</span><br/>{conn_&#8203;close_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">error</span>"} | counter | Connections to the ipecho service made and closed, by outcome (Closed abnormally) |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;conn_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes received on connections to the ipecho service |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;conn_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes sent on connections to the ipecho service |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;port_&#8203;check_&#8203;udp_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | Port check UDP packets sent to the joiner's UDP ports |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">connected</span>"} | counter | Port check TCP connections to the joiner's TCP ports, by result (Port check connections connected to the joiner's port) |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">failed</span>"} | counter | Port check TCP connections to the joiner's TCP ports, by result (Port check connections refused or errored) |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">timed_&#8203;out</span>"} | counter | Port check TCP connections to the joiner's TCP ports, by result (Port check connections timed out) |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">socket_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Port check TCP connections to the joiner's TCP ports, by result (Port check connections not attempted because the socket could not be created) |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">rejected_&#8203;per_&#8203;ip</span>"} | counter | Port check TCP connections to the joiner's TCP ports, by result (Port check connections rejected because too many were in flight for a particular IP) |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">evicted</span>"} | counter | Port check TCP connections to the joiner's TCP ports, by result (Port check connections evicted because there were too many in flight connections) |
| <span class="metrics-name">ipecho_&#8203;port_&#8203;check_&#8203;tcp_&#8203;in_&#8203;flight</span> | gauge | Port check TCP connections in flight |

</div>

## Snapct Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">snapct_&#8203;load_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Snapshot load controller state: 0=init (Initializing), 1=waiting_for_peers, 2=waiting_for_peers_incremental (Waiting for peers (incremental)), 3=collecting_peers, 4=collecting_peers_incremental (Collecting peers (incremental)), 5=reading_full_file (Reading full snapshot file), 6=flushing_full_file_fini (Full file read, draining downstream), 7=flushing_full_file_done (Full file loaded, draining downstream), 8=flushing_full_file_reset (Full file load aborted, resetting downstream), 9=reading_incremental_file (Reading incremental snapshot file), 10=flushing_incremental_file_fini (Incremental file read, draining downstream), 11=flushing_incremental_file_done (Incremental file loaded, draining downstream), 12=flushing_incremental_file_reset (Incremental file load aborted, resetting downstream), 13=reading_full_http (Downloading full snapshot), 14=flushing_full_http_fini (Full download read, draining downstream), 15=flushing_full_http_done (Full download loaded, draining downstream), 16=flushing_full_http_reset (Full download aborted, resetting downstream), 17=reading_incremental_http (Downloading incremental snapshot), 18=flushing_incremental_http_fini (Incremental download read, draining downstream), 19=flushing_incremental_http_done (Incremental download loaded, draining downstream), 20=flushing_incremental_http_reset (Incremental download aborted, resetting downstream), 21=shutdown (Snapshot load complete) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapct_&#8203;read_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | gauge | Snapshot file bytes read (download or local file) in the current attempt, by type; resets on retry (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapct_&#8203;read_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | gauge | Snapshot file bytes read (download or local file) in the current attempt, by type; resets on retry (Incremental snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapct_&#8203;read_&#8203;expected_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | gauge | Size of each snapshot file being loaded; may change if the load restarts (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapct_&#8203;read_&#8203;expected_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | gauge | Size of each snapshot file being loaded; may change if the load restarts (Incremental snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapct_&#8203;save_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | gauge | Bytes of each downloaded snapshot saved to the snapshot directory so far; resets if the load restarts (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapct_&#8203;save_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | gauge | Bytes of each downloaded snapshot saved to the snapshot directory so far; resets if the load restarts (Incremental snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapct_&#8203;load_&#8203;retried</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | counter | Snapshot downloads retried, by snapshot type (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapct_&#8203;load_&#8203;retried</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | counter | Snapshot downloads retried, by snapshot type (Incremental snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapct_&#8203;predicted_&#8203;incremental_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Slot of the incremental snapshot replay is predicted to start from, 0 if none chosen; may change if the load restarts |

</div>

## Snapdc Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">snapdc_&#8203;stream_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Snapshot decompressor state: 0=idle, 1=processing, 2=finishing, 3=error, 4=shutdown |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapdc_&#8203;compressed_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | gauge | Compressed bytes of each snapshot read in the current attempt; sum across snapdc tiles (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapdc_&#8203;compressed_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | gauge | Compressed bytes of each snapshot read in the current attempt; sum across snapdc tiles (Incremental snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapdc_&#8203;decompressed_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | gauge | Decompressed bytes of each snapshot produced in the current attempt; sum across snapdc tiles (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapdc_&#8203;decompressed_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | gauge | Decompressed bytes of each snapshot produced in the current attempt; sum across snapdc tiles (Incremental snapshot) |

</div>

## Snapin Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">snapin_&#8203;stream_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Snapshot inserter state: 0=idle, 1=processing, 2=finishing, 3=error, 4=shutdown |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapin_&#8203;parse_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | gauge | Decompressed bytes of each snapshot this tile parsed in the current attempt; every snapin tile parses the whole stream, so take the min across tiles (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapin_&#8203;parse_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | gauge | Decompressed bytes of each snapshot this tile parsed in the current attempt; every snapin tile parses the whole stream, so take the min across tiles (Incremental snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapin_&#8203;disk_&#8203;written_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes this tile has written to the accounts database file |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapin_&#8203;account_&#8203;progress</span><br/>{snapin_&#8203;account_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">new</span>"} | gauge | Accounts this tile processed in the current load, by result (Ignored accounts are not inserted); sum across tiles, rewinds on retry (New account inserted) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapin_&#8203;account_&#8203;progress</span><br/>{snapin_&#8203;account_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">replaced</span>"} | gauge | Accounts this tile processed in the current load, by result (Ignored accounts are not inserted); sum across tiles, rewinds on retry (Replaced an older duplicate inserted earlier) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapin_&#8203;account_&#8203;progress</span><br/>{snapin_&#8203;account_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">ignored</span>"} | gauge | Accounts this tile processed in the current load, by result (Ignored accounts are not inserted); sum across tiles, rewinds on retry (Dropped because an inserted duplicate was newer) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapin_&#8203;account_&#8203;processed</span> | counter | Accounts processed across all snapshots |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapin_&#8203;account_&#8203;batch_&#8203;processed</span> | counter | Account batches processed across all snapshots |

</div>

## Netlnk Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;rx_&#8203;buffer_&#8203;overflowed</span> | counter | Netlink receive buffer overflows (ENOBUFS); each forces a full resync |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;link_&#8203;table_&#8203;synced</span> | counter | Full link table syncs |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;route_&#8203;table_&#8203;synced</span> | counter | Full route table syncs |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;update_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{netlink_&#8203;message_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">link</span>"} | counter | Netlink live updates processed, by message type (Link) |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;update_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{netlink_&#8203;message_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">neighbor</span>"} | counter | Netlink live updates processed, by message type (Neighbor Table Entry) |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;update_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{netlink_&#8203;message_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">ipv4_&#8203;route</span>"} | counter | Netlink live updates processed, by message type (IPv4 Route Table Entry) |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;interface_&#8203;count</span> | gauge | Network interfaces in the interface table |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;requested</span><br/>{neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">sent</span>"} | counter | Neighbor solicit requests from net tiles, by result (Neighbor solicit sent to the kernel) |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;requested</span><br/>{neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">in_&#8203;flight</span>"} | counter | Neighbor solicit requests from net tiles, by result (Kernel already resolving this neighbor) |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;requested</span><br/>{neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">table_&#8203;full</span>"} | counter | Neighbor solicit requests from net tiles, by result (Neighbor table had no room for a placeholder entry) |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;requested</span><br/>{neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">send_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Neighbor solicit requests from net tiles, by result (Netlink send failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;requested</span><br/>{neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">host_&#8203;rate_&#8203;limited</span>"} | counter | Neighbor solicit requests from net tiles, by result (Per-host probe rate limit hit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">netlnk_&#8203;neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;requested</span><br/>{neighbor_&#8203;probe_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">global_&#8203;rate_&#8203;limited</span>"} | counter | Neighbor solicit requests from net tiles, by result (Global probe rate limit hit) |

</div>

## Net Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Packets successfully published to output links |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes of packets successfully published to output links |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">malformed</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (Undersized, oversized, or malformed Ethernet, IPv4 or UDP headers) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">dst_&#8203;ip_&#8203;mismatch</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (Destination IP does not match the bind address) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">dst_&#8203;port_&#8203;unbound</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (No output link for the destination UDP port) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">gre_&#8203;invalid</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (GRE tunnel peer or headers invalid) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">gre_&#8203;unconfigured</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (GRE packet with no GRE tunnel configured) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;rx_&#8203;fill_&#8203;ring_&#8203;stalled</span> | counter | Times RX stalled because the fill ring had no room; the packet stays in the RX ring and is retried (kernel drops are XdpRxRingFullDropped) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;rx_&#8203;buffer_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Receive buffers currently in use |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;rx_&#8203;buffer_&#8203;free</span> | gauge | Receive buffers currently free |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;submitted</span> | counter | Packet transmit jobs submitted |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;completed</span> | counter | Packet transmit jobs marked as completed by the kernel |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;submitted_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes of packets submitted for transmission |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (Invalid packet headers, or GRE packet exceeding the MTU) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;route</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (No matching route) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">unsupported_&#8203;route</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (Unsupported route type) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">interface_&#8203;missing</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (Interface not available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">unsupported_&#8203;interface</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (Interface type not supported) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">source_&#8203;ip_&#8203;missing</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (No source IP address chosen) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;neighbor</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (Next-hop neighbor unresolved) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">ring_&#8203;full</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (TX ring full or no TX buffer available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">gre_&#8203;no_&#8203;route</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (GRE tunnel addresses missing or outer route is itself tunnelled) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;tx_&#8203;buffer_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Transmit buffers currently in use |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;tx_&#8203;buffer_&#8203;free</span> | gauge | Transmit buffers currently free |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;xsk_&#8203;syscall_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | XSK sendto syscalls dispatched; in busy-poll mode each also counts in XskSyscallRx |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;xsk_&#8203;syscall_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | XSK recvmsg syscalls dispatched |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;xdp_&#8203;rx_&#8203;other_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Dropped for other reasons (xdp_statistics_v0.rx_dropped) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;xdp_&#8203;rx_&#8203;invalid_&#8203;descriptor_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Dropped due to invalid descriptor (xdp_statistics_v0.rx_invalid_descs) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;xdp_&#8203;tx_&#8203;invalid_&#8203;descriptor_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Dropped due to invalid descriptor (xdp_statistics_v0.tx_invalid_descs) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;xdp_&#8203;rx_&#8203;ring_&#8203;full_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Dropped due to rx ring being full (xdp_statistics_v1.rx_ring_full) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;xdp_&#8203;rx_&#8203;fill_&#8203;ring_&#8203;empty</span> | counter | Times the kernel found the fill ring empty (xdp_statistics_v1.rx_fill_ring_empty_descs) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;xdp_&#8203;tx_&#8203;ring_&#8203;empty</span> | counter | Times the kernel found the TX ring empty (xdp_statistics_v1.tx_ring_empty_descs) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;gre_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | GRE packets decapsulated and published; subset of PktRx |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;gre_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;submitted</span> | counter | GRE packet transmit jobs submitted; subset of PktTxSubmitted |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;route_&#8203;count</span><br/>{route_&#8203;table_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">local</span>"} | gauge | IPv4 routes installed in the forwarding table (Local) |
| <span class="metrics-name">net_&#8203;route_&#8203;count</span><br/>{route_&#8203;table_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">main</span>"} | gauge | IPv4 routes installed in the forwarding table (Main) |

</div>

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">sock_&#8203;syscall_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{sendmmsg_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | sendmmsg syscalls dispatched, by result (Success) |

| <span class="metrics-name">sock_&#8203;syscall_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{sendmmsg_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unreachable</span>"} | counter | sendmmsg syscalls dispatched, by result (ENETUNREACH, EHOSTUNREACH error) |
| <span class="metrics-name">sock_&#8203;syscall_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{sendmmsg_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">down</span>"} | counter | sendmmsg syscalls dispatched, by result (ENONET, ENETDOWN, EHOSTDOWN error) |
| <span class="metrics-name">sock_&#8203;syscall_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{sendmmsg_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">other</span>"} | counter | sendmmsg syscalls dispatched, by result (Unrecognized error code) |
| <span class="metrics-name">sock_&#8203;syscall_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | recvmmsg syscalls dispatched |
| <span class="metrics-name">sock_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Packets received |
| <span class="metrics-name">sock_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | Packets sent |
| <span class="metrics-name">sock_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;failed</span> | counter | Packets that failed to send |
| <span class="metrics-name">sock_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes of packets successfully sent |
| <span class="metrics-name">sock_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | UDP payload bytes received plus 42 bytes of synthesized Ethernet/IPv4/UDP headers per packet |

## Quic Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;reassembly_&#8203;evicted</span> | counter | Txns evicted from reassembly before completing (txn_reassembly_count too small) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;reassembly_&#8203;started</span> | counter | Fragmented txn receive ops started |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;reassembly_&#8203;pending</span> | gauge | Fragmented txn reassemblies in progress |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;reassembly_&#8203;chunk_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Txn stream chunks received for reassembly |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;reassembly_&#8203;gap_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Txn stream chunks dropped because of a data gap |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;reassembly_&#8203;duplicate_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Txn stream chunks dropped as duplicates of a completed stream |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{tpu_&#8203;rx_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">udp</span>"} | counter | Transactions published to verify, by TPU ingress path; excludes all *Dropped (TPU/UDP) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{tpu_&#8203;rx_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">quic_&#8203;fast</span>"} | counter | Transactions published to verify, by TPU ingress path; excludes all *Dropped (TPU/QUIC unfragmented) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{tpu_&#8203;rx_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">quic_&#8203;frag</span>"} | counter | Transactions published to verify, by TPU ingress path; excludes all *Dropped (TPU/QUIC fragmented) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;reassembly_&#8203;abandoned</span> | counter | Txn reassemblies abandoned because the connection was lost |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;undersize_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Txns received via QUIC dropped because they were too small |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;txn_&#8203;oversize_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Txns received via QUIC dropped because they were too large |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;udp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;undersize_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Packets received on the non-QUIC port that were too small to be a valid IP packet |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;udp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;oversize_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Packets received on the non-QUIC port that were too large to be a valid transaction |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | UDP datagrams received on the QUIC port (one may carry several coalesced QUIC packets) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes of UDP datagrams received on the QUIC port, including IP and UDP headers |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | IP packets sent |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes sent (including IP, UDP, QUIC headers) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">handshake</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Handshaking peer) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">handshake_&#8203;complete</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Handshake complete, confirming with peer) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">active</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Active connection) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">peer_&#8203;close</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Peer requested close) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">abort</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Connection terminating due to error) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">close_&#8203;pending</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Connection is closing) |

| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;free</span> | gauge | Connection slots free |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Connection slots |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;created</span> | counter | Connections created |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;closed</span> | counter | Connections gracefully closed |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;aborted</span> | counter | Connections aborted |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;timed_&#8203;out</span> | counter | Connections timed out |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;retried</span> | counter | Connections established with retry |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;no_&#8203;slot_&#8203;rejected</span> | counter | Connections that failed to create due to lack of slots |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;retry_&#8203;rejected</span> | counter | Connections that failed during retry (e.g. invalid token) |

| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">oversize</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (QUIC packet too large) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">src_&#8203;ip_&#8203;mismatch</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (Wrong source IP) |

| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">streams_&#8203;blocked</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (STREAMS_BLOCKED(bidi) frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">new_&#8203;conn_&#8203;id</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (NEW_CONN_ID frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">retire_&#8203;conn_&#8203;id</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (RETIRE_CONN_ID frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">path_&#8203;challenge</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (PATH_CHALLENGE frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">path_&#8203;response</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (PATH_RESPONSE frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">conn_&#8203;close_&#8203;quic</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (CONN_CLOSE(transport) frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">conn_&#8203;close_&#8203;app</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (CONN_CLOSE(app) frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">handshake_&#8203;done</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (HANDSHAKE_DONE frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">ping</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (PING frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">padding</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (PADDING frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">datagram</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (DATAGRAM frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;ack_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{ack_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;number</span>"} | counter | Received packets processed for acknowledgement, by result (Packet had no packet number to acknowledge) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;ack_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{ack_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">new_&#8203;range</span>"} | counter | Received packets processed for acknowledgement, by result (New ACK range) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;ack_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{ack_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">merged</span>"} | counter | Received packets processed for acknowledgement, by result (Merged into existing ACK range) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;ack_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{ack_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">queue_&#8203;full</span>"} | counter | Received packets processed for acknowledgement, by result (Out of buffers) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;ack_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{ack_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">cancelled</span>"} | counter | Received packets processed for acknowledgement, by result (ACK suppressed by handler) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;service_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of one fd_quic_service call that processed a due connection timer |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of processing one received packet |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;parse_&#8203;failed</span> | counter | QUIC frames that failed to parse |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;decrypt_&#8203;failed</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">initial</span>"} | counter | Packets that failed decryption, by encryption level (Initial) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;decrypt_&#8203;failed</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">early</span>"} | counter | Packets that failed decryption, by encryption level (Early data) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;decrypt_&#8203;failed</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">handshake</span>"} | counter | Packets that failed decryption, by encryption level (Handshake) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;decrypt_&#8203;failed</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">app</span>"} | counter | Packets that failed decryption, by encryption level (App data) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;no_&#8203;key_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">initial</span>"} | counter | Packets dropped because the decryption key was missing, by encryption level (Initial) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;no_&#8203;key_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">early</span>"} | counter | Packets dropped because the decryption key was missing, by encryption level (Early data) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;no_&#8203;key_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">handshake</span>"} | counter | Packets dropped because the decryption key was missing, by encryption level (Handshake) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;no_&#8203;key_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">app</span>"} | counter | Packets dropped because the decryption key was missing, by encryption level (App data) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;retry_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | QUIC Retry packets sent |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;retransmitted</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">initial</span>"} | counter | QUIC packets retransmitted, by encryption level (Initial) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;retransmitted</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">early</span>"} | counter | QUIC packets retransmitted, by encryption level (Early data) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;retransmitted</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">handshake</span>"} | counter | QUIC packets retransmitted, by encryption level (Handshake) |
| <span class="metrics-name">quic_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;retransmitted</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">app</span>"} | counter | QUIC packets retransmitted, by encryption level (App data) |

</div>

## Bundle Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Transactions received, including transactions within bundles |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;standalone_&#8203;txn_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Transactions received outside of a bundle |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;protobuf_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes from received gRPC protobuf payloads |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;bundle_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Bundles received |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;client_&#8203;failed</span><br/>{bundle_&#8203;client_&#8203;failure_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">decode</span>"} | counter | Bundle client failures, by cause (Protobuf decode or encode failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;client_&#8203;failed</span><br/>{bundle_&#8203;client_&#8203;failure_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">transport</span>"} | counter | Bundle client failures, by cause (gRPC transport failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;client_&#8203;failed</span><br/>{bundle_&#8203;client_&#8203;failure_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;fee_&#8203;info</span>"} | counter | Bundle client failures, by cause (Bundle dropped due to missing fee info) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;heap_&#8203;capacity_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Workspace heap capacity |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;heap_&#8203;free_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Approximate free space in workspace |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;ping_&#8203;acked</span> | counter | HTTP/2 PINGs acknowledged by server |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Block engine connection state; Sleeping means closed on purpose because the next leader slot is far away: 0=disconnected, 1=connecting, 2=connected, 3=sleeping |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;last_&#8203;rtt_&#8203;nanos</span> | gauge | Latest block engine HTTP/2 PING round trip, 0 if none since connect |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;rtt_&#8203;smoothed_&#8203;nanos</span> | gauge | Smoothed block engine HTTP/2 PING round trip (RFC 9002), 0 if none since connect |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;rtt_&#8203;deviation_&#8203;nanos</span> | gauge | Mean deviation of block engine HTTP/2 PING round trips (RFC 9002 rttvar), 0 if none since connect |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;message_&#8203;rx_&#8203;delay_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Local wall clock minus server timestamp per block engine message; includes clock skew, negative clamped to 0 |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;pending</span> | gauge | Transactions buffered and waiting to be published |
| <span class="metrics-name">bundle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;buffer_&#8203;full_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Transactions dropped because the pending transaction buffer was full |

</div>

## Resolv Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;bank</span>"} | counter | Transactions dropped, by reason (No bank was available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">blockhash_&#8203;expired</span>"} | counter | Transactions dropped, by reason (Blockhash expired) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">bundle_&#8203;peer_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Transactions dropped, by reason (Another transaction in the bundle failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;stash_&#8203;txn_&#8203;inserted</span> | counter | Transactions with an unknown blockhash added to the stash |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;stash_&#8203;txn_&#8203;removed</span><br/>{stash_&#8203;remove_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">published</span>"} | counter | Stashed transactions removed, by reason (Published once its blockhash became known) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;stash_&#8203;txn_&#8203;removed</span><br/>{stash_&#8203;remove_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">evicted</span>"} | counter | Stashed transactions removed, by reason (Evicted as the oldest entry to make room for a new one) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;stash_&#8203;txn_&#8203;removed</span><br/>{stash_&#8203;remove_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">failed</span>"} | counter | Stashed transactions removed, by reason (Removed without publishing because LUT resolution failed or no bank was available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (The transaction referenced an index in a LUT that didn't exist) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;uninitialized</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (The account referenced as a LUT hasn't been initialized) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;account_&#8203;data</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (The account referenced as a LUT couldn't be parsed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;account_&#8203;owner</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (The account referenced as a LUT wasn't owned by the ALUT program ID) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (The account referenced as a LUT couldn't be found) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (Resolved successfully) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (0-128 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (129-512 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (513 B-2 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le8_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (2-8 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (32-128 KiB) |

| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (513 B-2 KiB) |

| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolv_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
