## Pack Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;microblock_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of scheduling one microblock |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;microblock_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;empty_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of discovering that no transaction is schedulable |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of inserting one transaction into the pending pool |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;microblock_&#8203;complete_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of marking one microblock as complete |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;per_&#8203;microblock</span> | histogram | Transactions in a scheduled microblock, including both votes and non-votes |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;vote_&#8203;per_&#8203;microblock</span> | histogram | Simple vote transactions in a scheduled microblock |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Transactions received from resolv, including bundle members |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">instr_&#8203;too_&#8203;many_&#8203;accounts</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction has an instruction that references too many accounts) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonce_&#8203;conflict</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Bundle with two conflicting durable nonce transactions) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;blocklisted</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction uses an account on the account blocklist) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bundle_&#8203;blocklisted</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction is a vote in a bundle or uses an account on the bundle blocklist) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;nonce</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction is an invalid durable nonce transaction) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">write_&#8203;sysvar</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction tries to write to a sysvar) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">estimation_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Estimating compute cost or fee failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">duplicate_&#8203;account</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction included an account address twice) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;many_&#8203;accounts</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction tried to load too many accounts) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;many_&#8203;cu</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction requests too many CUs) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">expired</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction already expired) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">lut_&#8203;unsupported</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction loads accounts from a lookup table, which is not supported here) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unaffordable</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Fee payer's balance below transaction fee) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">duplicate</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Pack aware of transaction with same signature) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonce_&#8203;priority_&#8203;too_&#8203;low</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction's fee was too low given its compute unit requirement and another competing transactions that uses the same durable nonce) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">priority_&#8203;too_&#8203;low</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction's fee was too low given its compute unit requirement and other competing transactions) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonvote_&#8203;add</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction that was not a simple vote added to pending transactions) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">vote_&#8203;add</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Simple vote transaction was added to pending transactions) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonvote_&#8203;replace</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Transaction that was not a simple vote replaced a lower priority transaction) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">vote_&#8203;replace</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Simple vote transaction replaced a lower priority transaction) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonce_&#8203;nonvote_&#8203;add</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Durable nonce transaction added to pending transactions) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unused</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Never produced: durable nonce transactions can't be simple votes) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;insert_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonce_&#8203;nonvote_&#8203;replace</span>"} | counter | Insert attempts, by result; bundle members are counted individually with the bundle's result (Durable nonce transaction replaced a lower priority transaction, likely one that uses the same durable nonce) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">none</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (No microblock: not leader, no transactions, no available execle) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">txn</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (No microblock: not leader, transactions available, no available execle) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">execle</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (No microblock: not leader, no transactions, execle available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">txn_&#8203;execle</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (No microblock: not leader, transactions available, execle available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">leader</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (No microblock: leader, no transactions, no available execle) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">txn_&#8203;leader</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (No microblock: leader, transactions available, no available execle) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">execle_&#8203;leader</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (No microblock: leader, no transactions, execle available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">txn_&#8203;execle_&#8203;leader</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (No microblock: leader, transactions available, execle available (all conflicting)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">microblock</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (Scheduled a microblock: not leader, no transactions, no available execle) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">txn_&#8203;microblock</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (Scheduled a microblock: not leader, transactions available, no available execle) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">execle_&#8203;microblock</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (Scheduled a microblock: not leader, no transactions, execle available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">txn_&#8203;execle_&#8203;microblock</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (Scheduled a microblock: not leader, transactions available, execle available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">leader_&#8203;microblock</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (Scheduled a microblock: leader, no transactions, no available execle) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">txn_&#8203;leader_&#8203;microblock</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (Scheduled a microblock: leader, transactions available, no available execle) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">execle_&#8203;leader_&#8203;microblock</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (Scheduled a microblock: leader, no transactions, execle available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">txn_&#8203;execle_&#8203;leader_&#8203;microblock</span>"} | counter | Time pack spent, by scheduling regime (Scheduled a microblock: leader, transactions available, execle available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">expired</span>"} | counter | Transactions dropped from pack after insertion, by reason (TTL expired) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">already_&#8203;executed</span>"} | counter | Transactions dropped from pack after insertion, by reason (Already executed in the replay or leader pipeline) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">partial_&#8203;bundle</span>"} | counter | Transactions dropped from pack after insertion, by reason (Part of a partial bundle) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;evicted</span> | counter | Transactions evicted from pack to make room for others, including the bundle initializer transaction removed when a leader slot ends |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;pending_&#8203;used</span><br/>{pending_&#8203;txn_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">regular</span>"} | gauge | Pending transactions in pack's pool, by type (Non-votes in the main treap) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;pending_&#8203;used</span><br/>{pending_&#8203;txn_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">vote</span>"} | gauge | Pending transactions in pack's pool, by type (Simple votes) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;pending_&#8203;used</span><br/>{pending_&#8203;txn_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">conflicting</span>"} | gauge | Pending transactions in pack's pool, by type (Non-votes that write to a hotly-contended account) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;pending_&#8203;used</span><br/>{pending_&#8203;txn_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">bundle</span>"} | gauge | Pending transactions in pack's pool, by type (Transactions that are part of a bundle) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;pending_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Maximum pending transactions pack can hold |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;pending_&#8203;smallest_&#8203;cost</span> | gauge | Lower bound on the cost of the cheapest pending non-vote transaction, 0 if none pending |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;block_&#8203;microblock_&#8203;limit_&#8203;reached</span> | counter | Times pack did not schedule a microblock because the block microblock limit was reached |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;block_&#8203;byte_&#8203;limit_&#8203;reached</span> | counter | Times pack did not schedule a microblock because the block byte limit was reached |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">taken</span>"} | counter | Times a pending transaction was considered for a microblock, by outcome; one transaction may be considered many times (Pack included the transaction in the microblock) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">cu_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Times a pending transaction was considered for a microblock, by outcome; one transaction may be considered many times (Pack skipped the transaction because it would have exceeded the block CU limit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">conflict_&#8203;fast</span>"} | counter | Times a pending transaction was considered for a microblock, by outcome; one transaction may be considered many times (Pack skipped the transaction because of an account conflict found by the fast bitvector check) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">byte_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Times a pending transaction was considered for a microblock, by outcome; one transaction may be considered many times (Pack skipped the transaction because it would have exceeded the block data size limit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">alloc_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Times a pending transaction was considered for a microblock, by outcome; one transaction may be considered many times (Pack skipped the transaction because it would have exceeded the data allocation limit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">write_&#8203;cost</span>"} | counter | Times a pending transaction was considered for a microblock, by outcome; one transaction may be considered many times (Pack skipped the transaction because it would have caused a writable account to exceed the per-account block write cost limit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">conflict_&#8203;slow</span>"} | counter | Times a pending transaction was considered for a microblock, by outcome; one transaction may be considered many times (Pack skipped the transaction because of an account conflict found by the full check) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pack_&#8203;txn_&#8203;schedule_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">write_&#8203;cost_&#8203;deferred</span>"} | counter | Times a pending transaction was considered for a microblock, by outcome; one transaction may be considered many times (Pack skipped the transaction because it exceeded the per-account block write cost limit too many times before) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;bundle_&#8203;crank_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{bundle_&#8203;crank_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">up_&#8203;to_&#8203;date</span>"} | counter | Times pack checked whether the bundle tip state needed cranking, by outcome (On-chain state was already up to date) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;bundle_&#8203;crank_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{bundle_&#8203;crank_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">inserted</span>"} | counter | Times pack checked whether the bundle tip state needed cranking, by outcome (Inserted an initializer bundle to update the on-chain state) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;bundle_&#8203;crank_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{bundle_&#8203;crank_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">creation_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Times pack checked whether the bundle tip state needed cranking, by outcome (Tried to insert an initializer bundle to update the on-chain state, but creation failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;bundle_&#8203;crank_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{bundle_&#8203;crank_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">insertion_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Times pack checked whether the bundle tip state needed cranking, by outcome (Tried to insert an initializer bundle to update the on-chain state, but insertion failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;block_&#8203;cost</span> | gauge | Cost units used in the current block, 0 if not packing a block |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;cost_&#8203;scheduled_&#8203;per_&#8203;block</span> | histogram | Cost units scheduled for each block pack produced; can exceed the block limit because of rebates |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;cu_&#8203;rebated_&#8203;per_&#8203;block</span> | histogram | Compute units rebated for each block pack produced. Compute units are rebated when a transaction fails prior to execution or requests more compute units than it uses |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;cost_&#8203;net_&#8203;per_&#8203;block</span> | histogram | Net cost units (scheduled minus rebated) in each block pack produced |
| <span class="metrics-name">pack_&#8203;cost_&#8203;per_&#8203;block_&#8203;pct</span> | histogram | Percent of max_cost_per_block used by each block pack produced |

</div>

## Execle Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction executed successfully) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">instruction_&#8203;error</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (An error occurred while processing an instruction) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction fee payer address was not found) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">program_&#8203;account_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (A program account referenced by the transaction was not found) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">insufficient_&#8203;funds_&#8203;for_&#8203;fee</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction fee payer did not have balance to pay the fee) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;account_&#8203;for_&#8203;fee</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction fee payer account is not owned by the system program, or has data that is not a nonce) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">already_&#8203;processed</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction has already been processed in a recent block) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">blockhash_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references a blockhash that is not recent, or advances a nonce with the wrong value) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;program_&#8203;for_&#8203;execution</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (A program account referenced by the transaction was no executable.) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references an ALUT account that does not exist or is inactive) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;owner</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references an ALUT account that is not owned by the ALUT program account) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;data</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references an ALUT account that contains data which is not a valid ALUT) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references an account offset from the ALUT which does not exist) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">max_&#8203;loaded_&#8203;accounts_&#8203;data_&#8203;size_&#8203;exceeded</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The total account data size of the loaded accounts exceeds the consensus limit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">duplicate_&#8203;instruction</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (A compute budget program instruction was invoked more than once) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;loaded_&#8203;accounts_&#8203;data_&#8203;size_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The compute budget program was invoked and set the loaded accounts data size to zero) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonce_&#8203;already_&#8203;advanced</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references a nonce account that is already advanced) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonce_&#8203;advance_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction is a nonce transaction but the advance instruction was not valid or failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonce_&#8203;wrong_&#8203;blockhash</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction is a nonce transaction but the blockhash is not the correct one) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unsupported_&#8203;version</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction is a message version that is not supported by the current feature set) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">sanitize_&#8203;failure</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The process for sanitizing the transaction failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;in_&#8203;use</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction conflicts with another transaction in the microblock.) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;loaded_&#8203;twice</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references the same account twice) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">signature_&#8203;failure</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction had an invalid signature) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;many_&#8203;account_&#8203;locks</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references too many accounts.) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">insufficient_&#8203;funds_&#8203;for_&#8203;rent</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction would leave an account with a lower balance than the rent-exempt minimum) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unbalanced_&#8203;transaction</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The total referenced account lamports before and after the transaction was unbalanced) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bundle_&#8203;peer</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction was part of a bundle and an earlier transaction in the bundle failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;landing</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;landing_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by whether and how they landed in the block (Transaction landed and executed successfully) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;landing</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;landing_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">fees_&#8203;only</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by whether and how they landed in the block (Transaction landed, but was fees only and did not execute) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;landing</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;landing_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">failed</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by whether and how they landed in the block (Transaction landed, but failed to execute) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;landing</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;landing_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">dropped</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by whether and how they landed in the block (Transaction did not land) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;version</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;version="<span class="metrics-enum">legacy</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by transaction version (Legacy transaction format) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;version</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;version="<span class="metrics-enum">v0</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by transaction version (Version 0 transaction format) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;version</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;version="<span class="metrics-enum">v1</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by transaction version (Version 1 transaction format) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;instruction_&#8203;executed</span> | counter | Top-level instructions executed |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;cpi_&#8203;executed</span> | counter | Cross-program invocations executed |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;account_&#8203;lthash_&#8203;skipped</span> | counter | Committed writable accounts left byte-identical by their transaction, so their lthash update was skipped |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;cu_&#8203;consumed</span> | counter | Compute units consumed by executed transactions |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">load</span>"} | counter | Time per transaction, by phase; a phase counts only once the next phase starts, so dropped, fees-only and no-op transactions lose their trailing phases (Transaction checks and account loading) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">execute</span>"} | counter | Time per transaction, by phase; a phase counts only once the next phase starts, so dropped, fees-only and no-op transactions lose their trailing phases (Transaction execution, including VM setup) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;txn_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">commit</span>"} | counter | Time per transaction, by phase; a phase counts only once the next phase starts, so dropped, fees-only and no-op transactions lose their trailing phases (Transaction result commit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;vm_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{vm_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">setup</span>"} | counter | Time in top-level BPF invocations, by phase, with CPI setup and commit split out; nested programs, including native CPIs, count as Interpreter; subset of TxnRegime Execute (VM setup) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;vm_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{vm_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">commit</span>"} | counter | Time in top-level BPF invocations, by phase, with CPI setup and commit split out; nested programs, including native CPIs, count as Interpreter; subset of TxnRegime Execute (VM commit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;vm_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{vm_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">setup_&#8203;cpi</span>"} | counter | Time in top-level BPF invocations, by phase, with CPI setup and commit split out; nested programs, including native CPIs, count as Interpreter; subset of TxnRegime Execute (VM setup (CPI)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;vm_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{vm_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">commit_&#8203;cpi</span>"} | counter | Time in top-level BPF invocations, by phase, with CPI setup and commit split out; nested programs, including native CPIs, count as Interpreter; subset of TxnRegime Execute (VM commit (CPI)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;vm_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{vm_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">interpreter</span>"} | counter | Time in top-level BPF invocations, by phase, with CPI setup and commit split out; nested programs, including native CPIs, count as Interpreter; subset of TxnRegime Execute (VM interpreter execution) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;looked_&#8203;up</span> | counter | Program cache lookups; equals Hit + Miss + lookups of invalid program accounts |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;miss</span> | counter | Program cache misses, each followed by an insert or a spill |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;waited</span> | counter | Program cache lookups that waited on another tile's in-flight load of the same program |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;insert_&#8203;retried</span> | counter | Program cache inserts that found no free slot in their size class on the first try and retried after eviction |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;inserted_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes inserted into the program cache |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;spilled_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes loaded into program cache spill memory |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;evicted_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes evicted from the program cache by insert eviction sweeps |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Time in program cache lookups, including waits on peer loads and insert, eviction and ELF load on a miss; superset of ProgcacheLoadDurationNanos |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;loaded</span> | counter | Program ELF loads and verifications for the program cache, including failed verifications and spill-backed loads |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;load_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Time spent loading programs |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;hit</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Program cache hits, by size class (0-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;hit</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Program cache hits, by size class (128-512 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;hit</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Program cache hits, by size class (512 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;hit</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Program cache hits, by size class (1-2 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;hit</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le4_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Program cache hits, by size class (2-4 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;hit</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le11_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Program cache hits, by size class (4-11 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;inserted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Programs inserted into the program cache, by size class (0-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;inserted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Programs inserted into the program cache, by size class (128-512 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;inserted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Programs inserted into the program cache, by size class (512 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;inserted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Programs inserted into the program cache, by size class (1-2 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;inserted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le4_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Programs inserted into the program cache, by size class (2-4 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;inserted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le11_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Programs inserted into the program cache, by size class (4-11 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;evicted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Program cache slots reclaimed by an insert's eviction sweep, by size class (housekeeping preevicts are counted by the accdb tile) (0-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;evicted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Program cache slots reclaimed by an insert's eviction sweep, by size class (housekeeping preevicts are counted by the accdb tile) (128-512 KiB) |

| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;spilled</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Programs loaded into spill memory because no cache slot could be freed, by size class (128-512 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;spilled</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Programs loaded into spill memory because no cache slot could be freed, by size class (512 KiB-1 MiB) |

| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;overwrite_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Account versions committed over an existing version of the same fork and generation, by destination cache size class (128 KiB-1 MiB) |

| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (129-512 B) |

| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le8_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (2-8 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (8-32 KiB) |

| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;batch_&#8203;acquired</span> | counter | Batch acquire calls made by this tile |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;reserve_&#8203;retried</span> | counter | Spin retries while waiting to reserve cache lines for a transaction |
| <span class="metrics-name">execle_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes read from the account database file |

## Shred Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;dest_&#8203;per_&#8203;update</span> | histogram | Shred destinations in each cluster contact info update |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;batch_&#8203;abandoned</span> | counter | Microblock batches abandoned because we switched slots without finishing the current slot |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;parent_&#8203;block_&#8203;id_&#8203;zeroed</span> | counter | Leader blocks whose parent block_id was unknown, so the chained merkle root was zeroed |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;batch_&#8203;size_&#8203;bytes</span> | histogram | Size of each microblock batch that is shredded |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;microblock_&#8203;per_&#8203;batch</span> | histogram | Microblocks in each microblock batch that is shredded |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;batch_&#8203;shred_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of shredding one microblock batch |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;add_&#8203;shred_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of verifying and processing one shred received from the network |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;sync_&#8203;spill_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of synchronously spilling one FEC payload to the store |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;process_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">leader_&#8203;unknown</span>"} | counter | Shreds assigned to this tile that passed the initial parse and chained check, by processing result; InitialRejected and FecFatalRejected are subsets of Rejected (Shred was for a slot whose leader is unknown) |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;process_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">parse_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Shreds assigned to this tile that passed the initial parse and chained check, by processing result; InitialRejected and FecFatalRejected are subsets of Rejected (Shred parsing failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;process_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">equivocated</span>"} | counter | Shreds assigned to this tile that passed the initial parse and chained check, by processing result; InitialRejected and FecFatalRejected are subsets of Rejected (Shred was equivocated with another shred) |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;process_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">rejected</span>"} | counter | Shreds assigned to this tile that passed the initial parse and chained check, by processing result; InitialRejected and FecFatalRejected are subsets of Rejected (Shred was invalid for one of many reasons) |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;process_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">ignored</span>"} | counter | Shreds assigned to this tile that passed the initial parse and chained check, by processing result; InitialRejected and FecFatalRejected are subsets of Rejected (Shred was ignored because we had already reconstructed the FEC set, or was for an old slot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;process_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">duplicate</span>"} | counter | Shreds assigned to this tile that passed the initial parse and chained check, by processing result; InitialRejected and FecFatalRejected are subsets of Rejected (Shred was a duplicate of a previously received valid shred of an incomplete FEC set) |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;process_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">accepted</span>"} | counter | Shreds assigned to this tile that passed the initial parse and chained check, by processing result; InitialRejected and FecFatalRejected are subsets of Rejected (Shred accepted into an incomplete FEC set) |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;process_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">completed</span>"} | counter | Shreds assigned to this tile that passed the initial parse and chained check, by processing result; InitialRejected and FecFatalRejected are subsets of Rejected (Shred accepted and resulted in a valid, complete FEC set) |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;fec_&#8203;incomplete_&#8203;evicted</span> | counter | Incomplete FEC sets evicted from the resolver to make room for new ones |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;initial_&#8203;rejected</span> | counter | Shreds rejected before any resources were allocated for the FEC set |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;unchained_&#8203;rejected</span> | counter | Shreds rejected because they're not chained merkle shreds |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;fec_&#8203;fatal_&#8203;rejected</span> | counter | FEC sets rejected for reasons that cause the whole FEC set to become invalid |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;repair_&#8203;nonce_&#8203;failed</span> | counter | Shreds that looked like repair responses but had an incorrect nonce |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;repair_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Repair shreds received |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;repair_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes received from network packets with repair shreds, including network headers |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;turbine_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Turbine shreds received |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;turbine_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes received from network packets with turbine shreds, including network headers |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;repair_&#8203;completion_&#8203;assisted</span> | counter | FEC sets that completed with at least one repair shred contributing |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;repair_&#8203;lead_&#8203;delay_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Estimated time repair advanced FEC set completion: the delay from a repair-assisted completion until turbine had delivered as many shreds for that set as repair contributed. Counts arrivals rather than distinct indices, so turbine duplicates bias this low |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;sync_&#8203;spilled</span> | counter | FEC payloads the shred tile spilled to the store synchronously because the store RAM cache was full |
| <span class="metrics-name">shred_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;sync_&#8203;spilled_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | FEC payload bytes the shred tile spilled to the store synchronously |

</div>

| <span class="metrics-name">gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">dropped_&#8203;unparseable</span>"} | counter | Gossip messages received, by verification result (Unparseable) |

| <span class="metrics-name">gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">dropped_&#8203;pull_&#8203;request_&#8203;inactive</span>"} | counter | Gossip messages received, by verification result (Pull Request (inactive)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">dropped_&#8203;pull_&#8203;request_&#8203;wallclock</span>"} | counter | Gossip messages received, by verification result (Pull Request (wallclock)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">dropped_&#8203;pull_&#8203;request_&#8203;signature</span>"} | counter | Gossip messages received, by verification result (Pull Request (signature)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">dropped_&#8203;pull_&#8203;request_&#8203;shred_&#8203;version</span>"} | counter | Gossip messages received, by verification result (Pull Request (shred version)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{gossvf_&#8203;message_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">dropped_&#8203;pull_&#8203;request_&#8203;mask_&#8203;bits</span>"} | counter | Gossip messages received, by verification result (Pull Request (mask bits)) |

## Gossip Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;count</span><br/>{ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">unpinged</span>"} | gauge | Peers tracked for ping/pong, by state (Peer is known but has not been pinged yet) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;count</span><br/>{ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">awaiting_&#8203;pong</span>"} | gauge | Peers tracked for ping/pong, by state (Peer was pinged but has not yet responded, or responded with an invalid pong) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;count</span><br/>{ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">valid</span>"} | gauge | Peers tracked for ping/pong, by state (Peer was pinged and is currently valid) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;count</span><br/>{ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">refreshing</span>"} | gauge | Peers tracked for ping/pong, by state (Peer was pinged and is currently valid, and is being refreshed with a new ping) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;pong_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{pong_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">accepted</span>"} | counter | Pongs processed, by result (Pong accepted) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;pong_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{pong_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">staked</span>"} | counter | Pongs processed, by result (Ignored because the peer is staked and needs no ping) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;pong_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{pong_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">untracked</span>"} | counter | Pongs processed, by result (Ignored because the peer has no outstanding ping) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;pong_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{pong_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">address_&#8203;mismatch</span>"} | counter | Pongs processed, by result (Ignored because it came from a different address than the one pinged) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;pong_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{pong_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">token_&#8203;mismatch</span>"} | counter | Pongs processed, by result (Ignored because the token did not match) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;evicted</span> | counter | Peers evicted from the ping tracker to make room for new ones |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;added</span> | counter | Peers added to the ping tracker |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;staked_&#8203;removed</span> | counter | Peers removed from the ping tracker because they became staked |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;ping_&#8203;peer_&#8203;address_&#8203;changed</span> | counter | Tracked peers whose gossip address changed; pong invalidated and reset to unpinged |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;sign_&#8203;queue_&#8203;full_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Messages dropped because the maximum number of sign requests were in flight |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Capacity of the data store |
