| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">snapshot_&#8203;hashes</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Snapshot Hashes) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">accounts_&#8203;hashes</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Accounts Hashes) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">epoch_&#8203;slots</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Epoch Slots) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">version_&#8203;v1</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Version V1) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">version_&#8203;v2</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Version V2) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">node_&#8203;instance</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Node Instance) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">duplicate_&#8203;shred</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Duplicate Shred) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental_&#8203;snapshot_&#8203;hashes</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Incremental Snapshot Hashes) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">contact_&#8203;info_&#8203;v2</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Contact Info V2) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">restart_&#8203;last_&#8203;voted_&#8203;fork_&#8203;slots</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Restart Last Voted Fork Slots) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;crds_&#8203;pull_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span><br/>{crds_&#8203;value_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">restart_&#8203;heaviest_&#8203;fork</span>"} | counter | Wire bytes of CRDS values sent in pull response messages, by value type (Restart Heaviest Fork) |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;wait_&#8203;for_&#8203;supermajority_&#8203;staked_&#8203;peer_&#8203;online</span> | gauge | Staked peers with recent gossip activity |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;wait_&#8203;for_&#8203;supermajority_&#8203;staked_&#8203;peer_&#8203;count</span> | gauge | Staked peers tracked while waiting for supermajority |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;wait_&#8203;for_&#8203;supermajority_&#8203;stake_&#8203;online_&#8203;lamports</span> | gauge | Stake from peers with recent gossip activity |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;wait_&#8203;for_&#8203;supermajority_&#8203;stake_&#8203;tracked_&#8203;lamports</span> | gauge | Stake tracked while waiting for supermajority |
| <span class="metrics-name">gossip_&#8203;wait_&#8203;for_&#8203;supermajority_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Wait-for-supermajority progress: 0=unset (Not started), 1=loading_stakes (Loading stakes from the snapshot), 2=waiting_for_stake (Waiting for enough stake to come online), 3=publishing (Publishing our contact info to start), 4=done_or_disabled (Done, or wait-for-supermajority is disabled) |

</div>

## Repair Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | Network packets sent, including reqs, pings, pongs, etc |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">pong</span>"} | counter | Repair requests sent, by type (Pong) |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">window_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Repair requests sent, by type (Shred at an index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">highest_&#8203;window_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Repair requests sent, by type (Highest shred at or above an index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">orphan</span>"} | counter | Repair requests sent, by type (Ancestors of an orphan slot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">parent_&#8203;fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;count</span>"} | counter | Repair requests sent, by type (Parent and FEC set count of a block (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;root</span>"} | counter | Repair requests sent, by type (Merkle root of a FEC set (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">window_&#8203;index_&#8203;for_&#8203;block_&#8203;id</span>"} | counter | Repair requests sent, by type (Shred at an index of a specific block (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;highest_&#8203;repaired_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest slot on the repair frontier, 0 if none; its ancestors are complete, the slot itself may not be |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;highest_&#8203;turbine_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest slot a turbine shred was received for, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;shred_&#8203;old_&#8203;ignored</span> | counter | Shreds received older than the root, ignored |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;last_&#8203;requested_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Last slot we requested a single shred for, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;last_&#8203;requested_&#8203;orphan_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Last slot we requested an orphan for, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;in_&#8203;flight</span> | gauge | Requests awaiting a response, excluding orphan and highest window requests |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;peer_&#8203;count</span> | gauge | Repair peers currently tracked |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;sign_&#8203;backpressure</span> | counter | Loop iterations where no sign tile was available to sign a request |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;turbine_&#8203;grace_&#8203;elapsed</span> | counter | Times a missing shred outlived the turbine grace period and became eligible for repair |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;shred_&#8203;rerequested</span> | counter | Times we re-requested a shred from the inflights queue |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;ping_&#8203;rejected</span><br/>{ping_&#8203;reject_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">malformed</span>"} | counter | Pings rejected, by reason (Failed to decode) |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;ping_&#8203;rejected</span><br/>{ping_&#8203;reject_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">unknown_&#8203;peer</span>"} | counter | Pings rejected, by reason (Sender is not a known peer) |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;ping_&#8203;rejected</span><br/>{ping_&#8203;reject_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">signature_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Pings rejected, by reason (Signature did not verify) |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;slot_&#8203;complete_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Time from a slot's first shred (first request for catchup slots) until all its data shreds are received |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;response_&#8203;latency_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Time from sending a repair request to receiving its response |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;block_&#8203;evicted</span> | counter | Blocks evicted from the forest |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;block_&#8203;insert_&#8203;failed</span> | counter | Blocks we failed to insert into the forest due to failed eviction |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;last_&#8203;evicted_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Most recent slot evicted from the forest, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;last_&#8203;eviction_&#8203;cause_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Slot that caused the most recent forest eviction, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;last_&#8203;insert_&#8203;failed_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Most recent slot we failed to insert into the forest, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;chain_&#8203;verify_&#8203;failed</span> | counter | Times any block failed chained merkle verification |
| <span class="metrics-name">repair_&#8203;last_&#8203;chain_&#8203;verify_&#8203;failed_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Most recent slot that failed chained merkle verification, 0 if none |

</div>

## Rserve Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">pong</span>"} | counter | Requests with a supported tag and correct size (pongs on tag alone), by type, before identity, signature and ping checks (Pong) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">window_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Requests with a supported tag and correct size (pongs on tag alone), by type, before identity, signature and ping checks (Shred at an index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">highest_&#8203;window_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Requests with a supported tag and correct size (pongs on tag alone), by type, before identity, signature and ping checks (Highest shred at or above an index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">orphan</span>"} | counter | Requests with a supported tag and correct size (pongs on tag alone), by type, before identity, signature and ping checks (Ancestors of an orphan slot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">parent_&#8203;fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;count</span>"} | counter | Requests with a supported tag and correct size (pongs on tag alone), by type, before identity, signature and ping checks (Parent and FEC set count of a block (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;root</span>"} | counter | Requests with a supported tag and correct size (pongs on tag alone), by type, before identity, signature and ping checks (Merkle root of a FEC set (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">window_&#8203;index_&#8203;for_&#8203;block_&#8203;id</span>"} | counter | Requests with a supported tag and correct size (pongs on tag alone), by type, before identity, signature and ping checks (Shred at an index of a specific block (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes of incoming repair request payloads |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;malformed</span><br/>{request_&#8203;malformed_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;small</span>"} | counter | Packets dropped before parsing, by reason (Packet shorter than a message tag) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;malformed</span><br/>{request_&#8203;malformed_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">unsupported_&#8203;ping</span>"} | counter | Packets dropped before parsing, by reason (Ping sent to the repair server port) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;malformed</span><br/>{request_&#8203;malformed_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">unknown_&#8203;tag</span>"} | counter | Packets dropped before parsing, by reason (Unknown or unsupported message tag) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;malformed</span><br/>{request_&#8203;malformed_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">wrong_&#8203;size</span>"} | counter | Packets dropped before parsing, by reason (Message size does not match its tag) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;malformed</span><br/>{request_&#8203;malformed_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">unsupported_&#8203;ancestor_&#8203;hashes</span>"} | counter | Packets dropped before parsing, by reason (Ancestor hashes request, which is not served) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{request_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">signature_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Requests dropped during validation, by reason (Signature failed to verify) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{request_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">from_&#8203;self</span>"} | counter | Requests dropped during validation, by reason (Sent by our own identity) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{request_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">wrong_&#8203;recipient</span>"} | counter | Requests dropped during validation, by reason (Addressed to another identity) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{request_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">timestamp_&#8203;skew</span>"} | counter | Requests dropped during validation, by reason (Timestamp outside the accepted window) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{request_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">pong_&#8203;token_&#8203;invalid</span>"} | counter | Requests dropped during validation, by reason (Pong token outdated or invalid) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{request_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">shred_&#8203;index_&#8203;invalid</span>"} | counter | Requests dropped during validation, by reason (Shred index past the end of a block) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{request_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;index_&#8203;invalid</span>"} | counter | Requests dropped during validation, by reason (FEC set index unaligned or past the end of the block) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;unanswered</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">pong</span>"} | counter | Valid requests that could not be answered, by type (Pong) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;unanswered</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">window_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Valid requests that could not be answered, by type (Shred at an index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;unanswered</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">highest_&#8203;window_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Valid requests that could not be answered, by type (Highest shred at or above an index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;unanswered</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">orphan</span>"} | counter | Valid requests that could not be answered, by type (Ancestors of an orphan slot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;unanswered</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">parent_&#8203;fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;count</span>"} | counter | Valid requests that could not be answered, by type (Parent and FEC set count of a block (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;unanswered</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;root</span>"} | counter | Valid requests that could not be answered, by type (Merkle root of a FEC set (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;request_&#8203;unanswered</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">window_&#8203;index_&#8203;for_&#8203;block_&#8203;id</span>"} | counter | Valid requests that could not be answered, by type (Shred at an index of a specific block (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | Network packets sent, including pings and responses |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;response_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">ping</span>"} | counter | Response packets sent, by type; Orphan sends up to 11 per request (Ping sent to a requester not yet in the ping cache) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;response_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">window_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Response packets sent, by type; Orphan sends up to 11 per request (Shred at an index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;response_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">highest_&#8203;window_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Response packets sent, by type; Orphan sends up to 11 per request (Highest shred at or above an index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;response_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">orphan</span>"} | counter | Response packets sent, by type; Orphan sends up to 11 per request (Shred from an ancestor of an orphan slot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;response_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">parent_&#8203;fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;count</span>"} | counter | Response packets sent, by type; Orphan sends up to 11 per request (Parent and FEC set count of a block (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;response_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;root</span>"} | counter | Response packets sent, by type; Orphan sends up to 11 per request (Merkle root of a FEC set (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;response_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">window_&#8203;index_&#8203;for_&#8203;block_&#8203;id</span>"} | counter | Response packets sent, by type; Orphan sends up to 11 per request (Shred at an index of a specific block (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;response_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Payload bytes sent in response packets |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Store reads for repair responses, by result (Read served from the store) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">miss</span>"} | counter | Store reads for repair responses, by result (Not in the store) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">busy</span>"} | counter | Store reads for repair responses, by result (Index binding or ring cell changed during the read) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">scan_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Store reads for repair responses, by result (Cached highest-shred bound was no longer readable) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Shred bytes read from the store for repair responses |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;shred_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Shreds currently in the shreds database |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;shred_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Shreds the shreds database can hold |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;used_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Logical bytes occupied by live wire shreds and spilled FEC payloads |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;peak_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Logical wire-ring high-water plus spill pages high-water |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;write_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of persisting one accepted data shred |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;shred_&#8203;written</span> | counter | Data shreds persisted to the repair store |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;shred_&#8203;write_&#8203;failed</span> | counter | Data shreds rejected by the repair store API |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;disk_&#8203;shred_&#8203;written_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes written by persisted data shreds |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;preevict_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of preevicting one FEC payload from the store RAM cache |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;preevicted</span> | counter | FEC payloads preevicted from the store RAM cache to disk |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;preevicted_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | FEC payload bytes preevicted from the store RAM cache to disk |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;cache_&#8203;free</span> | gauge | Immediately available FEC payload cache entries |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;cache_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | FEC payload cache capacity |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;cache_&#8203;free_&#8203;target</span> | gauge | Free entries background preeviction tries to restore |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;cache_&#8203;free_&#8203;low_&#8203;water</span> | gauge | Free entry count below which background preeviction starts |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;ping_&#8203;cache_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Active entries in the ping cache |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;ping_&#8203;cache_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Ping cache entry capacity |
| <span class="metrics-name">rserve_&#8203;ping_&#8203;cache_&#8203;evicted</span> | counter | Ping cache entries evicted for a new peer (cache full); TTL expiries not counted |

</div>

## Replay Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;identity_&#8203;balance_&#8203;lamports</span> | gauge | Identity account balance at the optimistically confirmed slot |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;active_&#8203;stake_&#8203;lamports</span> | gauge | Our active stake at the optimistically confirmed slot |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;cluster_&#8203;active_&#8203;stake_&#8203;lamports</span> | gauge | Cluster active stake at the optimistically confirmed slot |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;epoch_&#8203;credits</span> | gauge | Our vote account epoch credits at the optimistically confirmed slot |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;is_&#8203;vote_&#8203;account_&#8203;inadmissible</span> | gauge | Whether our vote account's stake is not admitted: at the optimistically confirmed slot it fails the validator admission ticket filter (missing, below the V4 rent-exempt minimum, or not V4 with a BLS pubkey), or it passes but has not yet been admitted at an epoch boundary |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;last_&#8203;rewarded_&#8203;vote_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Latest slot for which our vote appears in a reward certificate, 0 if none observed (always 0 under Tower) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;store_&#8203;query_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of ingesting one queried FEC set into the scheduler |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;queried</span> | counter | FEC sets queried from the store |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;query_&#8203;missed</span> | counter | FEC set store queries that found no entry |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;spilled</span> | counter | FEC payloads spilled from the store RAM cache to disk |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;spilled_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | FEC payload bytes spilled from the store RAM cache to disk |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;spill_&#8203;read</span> | counter | Spilled FEC payloads read from disk |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;spill_&#8203;read_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Spilled FEC payload bytes read from disk |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;cache_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | FEC payload slots in use in the store RAM cache |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;cache_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | FEC payload capacity of the store RAM cache |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;store_&#8203;fec_&#8203;cache_&#8203;pinned</span> | gauge | FEC payload views currently pinned against eviction |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;is_&#8203;caught_&#8203;up</span> | gauge | Whether replay has caught up to the cluster tip; set once per run, never cleared |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;root_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Most recent consensus root, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;root_&#8203;slot_&#8203;distance</span> | gauge | Slots between the current reset slot and our root |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;leader_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Slot at which we are currently leader, or 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;next_&#8203;leader_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Slot at which we are next leader, or 0 if none. If we are currently leader, this is the same as the current leader slot |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;reset_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Slot replay last reset to (Tower reset slot; under Alpenglow the newest replayed slot or parent-ready parent); can decrease |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;bank_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Maximum number of banks we can have alive |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;bank_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Banks we currently have alive |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;reassembly_&#8203;free</span> | gauge | Free FEC sets in the reassembly queue |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;last_&#8203;reassembly_&#8203;dequeued_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Slot of the FEC set most recently dequeued from reassembly for replay, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;last_&#8203;reassembly_&#8203;dequeued_&#8203;fec_&#8203;index</span> | gauge | FEC set index of the FEC set most recently dequeued from reassembly for replay; read with LastReassemblyDequeuedSlot |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;active_&#8203;bank_&#8203;index</span> | gauge | Bank pool index the replay scheduler is dispatching work for, ULONG_MAX if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;last_&#8203;dispatch_&#8203;bank_&#8203;index</span> | gauge | Bank pool index the replay scheduler last dispatched work for, ULONG_MAX if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;last_&#8203;dispatch_&#8203;time</span> | gauge | Wall-clock time the replay scheduler last dispatched work, 0 if never |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;staging_&#8203;lane_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Staging lanes currently occupied in the replay scheduler. Up to four staging lanes can be occupied at once |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;staging_&#8203;lane_&#8203;peak</span> | gauge | High watermark of staging lanes that were at one point occupied in the replay scheduler |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;staging_&#8203;lane_&#8203;head_&#8203;bank_&#8203;index</span><br/>{staging_&#8203;lane="<span class="metrics-enum">lane0</span>"} | gauge | Bank pool index at the head of each replay staging lane, ULONG_MAX if the lane is empty (Staging lane 0) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;staging_&#8203;lane_&#8203;head_&#8203;bank_&#8203;index</span><br/>{staging_&#8203;lane="<span class="metrics-enum">lane1</span>"} | gauge | Bank pool index at the head of each replay staging lane, ULONG_MAX if the lane is empty (Staging lane 1) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;staging_&#8203;lane_&#8203;head_&#8203;bank_&#8203;index</span><br/>{staging_&#8203;lane="<span class="metrics-enum">lane2</span>"} | gauge | Bank pool index at the head of each replay staging lane, ULONG_MAX if the lane is empty (Staging lane 2) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;staging_&#8203;lane_&#8203;head_&#8203;bank_&#8203;index</span><br/>{staging_&#8203;lane="<span class="metrics-enum">lane3</span>"} | gauge | Bank pool index at the head of each replay staging lane, ULONG_MAX if the lane is empty (Staging lane 3) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;pool_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Transaction pool entries held by the replay scheduler, from parse until release |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;pool_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Maximum transactions the replay scheduler can hold |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;microblock_&#8203;pool_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Microblocks available to be scheduled in the replay scheduler |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;microblock_&#8203;pool_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Maximum microblocks the replay scheduler can hold |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;pool_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Blocks alive in the replay scheduler |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;pool_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Maximum blocks the replay scheduler can track |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;added</span><br/>{sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;staging_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">staged</span>"} | counter | Blocks added to the replay scheduler, by staging state (Added as staged) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;added</span><br/>{sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;staging_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">unstaged</span>"} | counter | Blocks added to the replay scheduler, by staging state (Added as unstaged) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;added</span><br/>{sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;staging_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">dead</span>"} | counter | Blocks added to the replay scheduler, by staging state (Added as the dead child of a dead block, never dispatched) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;replayed</span> | counter | Blocks the replay scheduler considers fully replayed |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;abandoned</span> | counter | Blocks the replay scheduler abandoned for any reason |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;rejected</span> | counter | Blocks the replay scheduler found invalid while parsing or verifying PoH; also counted in SchedBlockAbandoned once drained; replay-tile verdicts not counted |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;promoted</span> | counter | Blocks the replay scheduler promoted from unstaged to a staging lane |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;demoted</span> | counter | Blocks the replay scheduler demoted from a staging lane to unstaged |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;deactivated</span><br/>{sched_&#8203;deactivate_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;child</span>"} | counter | Times the replay scheduler stopped dispatching a block, by reason (A block fully replayed and there was no child block available yet) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;deactivated</span><br/>{sched_&#8203;deactivate_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;work</span>"} | counter | Times the replay scheduler stopped dispatching a block, by reason (A partially replayed block ran out of work to dispatch) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;deactivated</span><br/>{sched_&#8203;deactivate_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">abandoned</span>"} | counter | Times the replay scheduler stopped dispatching a block, by reason (A block was abandoned, other than by minority fork pruning) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;block_&#8203;deactivated</span><br/>{sched_&#8203;deactivate_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">minority_&#8203;fork</span>"} | counter | Times the replay scheduler stopped dispatching a block, by reason (A block was pruned for being on a minority fork (not also counted as Abandoned)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;lane_&#8203;switched</span> | counter | Times the replay scheduler switched the lane it is dispatching from |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;lane_&#8203;promoted</span> | counter | Times the replay scheduler promoted one or more blocks onto a staging lane |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;lane_&#8203;demoted</span> | counter | Times the replay scheduler demoted one or more blocks from a staging lane |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;fork_&#8203;observed</span> | counter | Forks the replay scheduler has observed |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{sched_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Transactions with address lookup tables the replay scheduler tried to resolve, by result (Address lookup tables expanded successfully) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{sched_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">failed</span>"} | counter | Transactions with address lookup tables the replay scheduler tried to resolve, by result (Address lookup table expansion failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;parsed_&#8203;abandoned</span> | counter | Transactions that were parsed but ended up being in abandoned blocks |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;executed_&#8203;abandoned</span> | counter | Transactions that were executed but ended up being in abandoned blocks |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;done_&#8203;abandoned</span> | counter | Transactions that were fully replayed but ended up being in abandoned blocks |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;in_&#8203;flight_&#8203;integral_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Integral of in-flight transactions over time (transaction-nanoseconds); divide by SchedTxnBusyDurationNanos for the average in-flight count |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;busy_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Time the replay scheduler had at least one transaction in flight |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;idle_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Time the replay scheduler had no transaction in flight |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;parsed</span> | counter | Transactions the replay pipeline has parsed |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;executed</span> | counter | Transactions the replay pipeline has executed |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;signature_&#8203;verified</span> | counter | Transactions the replay pipeline has sigverified |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;poh_&#8203;mixed</span> | counter | Transactions the replay pipeline has performed PoH mixin on |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;txn_&#8203;done</span> | counter | Transactions that were fully replayed |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;microblock_&#8203;parsed</span> | counter | Microblocks the replay pipeline has parsed |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;microblock_&#8203;hashed</span> | counter | Microblocks the replay pipeline has fully hashed |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;microblock_&#8203;done</span> | counter | Microblocks that were fully replayed |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;ingested_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes the replay scheduler has ingested |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;trailing_&#8203;skipped_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Trailing bytes of a block the replay scheduler ingested but did not parse |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;dropped_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes the replay scheduler refused to ingest because the block was abandoned |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;sched_&#8203;fec_&#8203;ingested</span> | counter | FEC sets the replay scheduler has been given |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;bank_&#8203;frozen</span> | counter | Banks frozen, including our own leader blocks |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;chain_&#8203;txn_&#8203;height</span> | gauge | Transactions from genesis through the last replayed block, 0 if none; drops on a fork switch |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;fec_&#8203;poll_&#8203;skipped</span><br/>{fec_&#8203;poll_&#8203;skip_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">sched_&#8203;full</span>"} | counter | Replay loop iterations that processed no FEC set, by reason (The scheduler was full) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;fec_&#8203;poll_&#8203;skipped</span><br/>{fec_&#8203;poll_&#8203;skip_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">reassembly_&#8203;empty</span>"} | counter | Replay loop iterations that processed no FEC set, by reason (The reassembly queue was empty) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;fec_&#8203;poll_&#8203;skipped</span><br/>{fec_&#8203;poll_&#8203;skip_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">leader_&#8203;freeze_&#8203;wait</span>"} | counter | Replay loop iterations that processed no FEC set, by reason (Waiting for PoH to end our leader block) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;fec_&#8203;poll_&#8203;skipped</span><br/>{fec_&#8203;poll_&#8203;skip_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">bank_&#8203;full</span>"} | counter | Replay loop iterations that processed no FEC set, by reason (The bank pool was full) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;fec_&#8203;parent_&#8203;missing_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | FEC sets dropped because replay could not resolve the block's parent (Alpenglow), which was dropped on a dead lineage, evicted with its tracking slot reused, or rooted past |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;fec_&#8203;rooted_&#8203;past_&#8203;skipped</span> | counter | Rotor FEC sets of a slot at or below the published root, or of a block whose parent is below it, skipped rather than dropped: no redelivery can replay them |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;root_&#8203;advance_&#8203;deferred</span> | counter | Times the storage root could not advance to the consensus root yet |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;leader_&#8203;window_&#8203;already_&#8203;produced</span> | counter | Leader windows given up because the cluster already produced one of their slots, eg. our own past slots replayed after a restart (Alpenglow) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;leader_&#8203;window_&#8203;superseded</span> | counter | Leader windows given up after votor sent them because certificates already let a later window start (Alpenglow) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;rooted</span> | counter | Program cache entries rooted |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;used</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value slots in use, by size class (0-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;used</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;kib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value slots in use, by size class (128-512 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;used</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value slots in use, by size class (512 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;used</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value slots in use, by size class (1-2 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;used</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le4_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value slots in use, by size class (2-4 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;used</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le11_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value slots in use, by size class (4-11 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;capacity</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value capacity, by size class (0-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;capacity</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;kib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value capacity, by size class (128-512 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;capacity</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value capacity, by size class (512 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;capacity</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value capacity, by size class (1-2 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;capacity</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le4_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value capacity, by size class (2-4 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;class_&#8203;capacity</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le11_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Program cache value capacity, by size class (4-11 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (0-128 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (129-512 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (513 B-2 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le8_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (2-8 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (32-128 KiB) |

| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;writable_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Writable accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;writable_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Writable accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;writable_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Writable accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (1-10 MiB) |

| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;evicted</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts evicted from the account database cache to make space, by cache size class (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;evicted</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Accounts evicted from the account database cache to make space, by cache size class (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;evicted</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Accounts evicted from the account database cache to make space, by cache size class (1-10 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;new_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | New account versions committed to the index, by destination cache size class (0-128 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;new_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | New account versions committed to the index, by destination cache size class (129-512 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;new_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | New account versions committed to the index, by destination cache size class (513 B-2 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;new_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le8_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | New account versions committed to the index, by destination cache size class (2-8 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;new_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | New account versions committed to the index, by destination cache size class (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;new_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | New account versions committed to the index, by destination cache size class (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;new_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | New account versions committed to the index, by destination cache size class (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;new_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | New account versions committed to the index, by destination cache size class (1-10 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;overwrite_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Account versions committed over an existing version of the same fork and generation, by destination cache size class (0-128 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;overwrite_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Account versions committed over an existing version of the same fork and generation, by destination cache size class (129-512 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;overwrite_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Account versions committed over an existing version of the same fork and generation, by destination cache size class (513 B-2 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;overwrite_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le8_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Account versions committed over an existing version of the same fork and generation, by destination cache size class (2-8 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;overwrite_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Account versions committed over an existing version of the same fork and generation, by destination cache size class (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;overwrite_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Account versions committed over an existing version of the same fork and generation, by destination cache size class (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;overwrite_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Account versions committed over an existing version of the same fork and generation, by destination cache size class (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;overwrite_&#8203;committed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Account versions committed over an existing version of the same fork and generation, by destination cache size class (1-10 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (0-128 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (129-512 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (513 B-2 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le8_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (2-8 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (1-10 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;waited</span> | counter | Accounts that had to wait for another reader to finish loading them into the cache |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;batch_&#8203;acquired</span> | counter | Batch acquire calls made by this tile |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;reserve_&#8203;retried</span> | counter | Spin retries while waiting to reserve cache lines for a transaction |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes read from the account database file |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read</span> | counter | Reads from the account database file |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;written_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes written to the account database file |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;written</span> | counter | Writes to the account database file |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;copied_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Account data bytes copied in memory between cache lines, staging buffers and callers; disk reads excluded |
| <span class="metrics-name">replay_&#8203;is_&#8203;runtime_&#8203;loaded</span> | gauge | Whether the runtime has loaded a bank and can replay |

</div>

## Execrp Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;is_&#8203;processing</span> | gauge | Whether the tile is currently processing a task (executing, sigverifying, or PoH hashing) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction executed successfully) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">instruction_&#8203;error</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (An error occurred while processing an instruction) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction fee payer address was not found) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">program_&#8203;account_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (A program account referenced by the transaction was not found) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">insufficient_&#8203;funds_&#8203;for_&#8203;fee</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction fee payer did not have balance to pay the fee) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;account_&#8203;for_&#8203;fee</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction fee payer account is not owned by the system program, or has data that is not a nonce) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">already_&#8203;processed</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction has already been processed in a recent block) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">blockhash_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references a blockhash that is not recent, or advances a nonce with the wrong value) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;program_&#8203;for_&#8203;execution</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (A program account referenced by the transaction was no executable.) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references an ALUT account that does not exist or is inactive) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;owner</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references an ALUT account that is not owned by the ALUT program account) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;data</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references an ALUT account that contains data which is not a valid ALUT) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references an account offset from the ALUT which does not exist) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">max_&#8203;loaded_&#8203;accounts_&#8203;data_&#8203;size_&#8203;exceeded</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The total account data size of the loaded accounts exceeds the consensus limit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">duplicate_&#8203;instruction</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (A compute budget program instruction was invoked more than once) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;loaded_&#8203;accounts_&#8203;data_&#8203;size_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The compute budget program was invoked and set the loaded accounts data size to zero) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonce_&#8203;already_&#8203;advanced</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references a nonce account that is already advanced) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonce_&#8203;advance_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction is a nonce transaction but the advance instruction was not valid or failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">nonce_&#8203;wrong_&#8203;blockhash</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction is a nonce transaction but the blockhash is not the correct one) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unsupported_&#8203;version</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction is a message version that is not supported by the current feature set) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">sanitize_&#8203;failure</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The process for sanitizing the transaction failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;in_&#8203;use</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction conflicts with another transaction in the microblock.) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;loaded_&#8203;twice</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references the same account twice) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">signature_&#8203;failure</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction had an invalid signature) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;many_&#8203;account_&#8203;locks</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction references too many accounts.) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">insufficient_&#8203;funds_&#8203;for_&#8203;rent</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction would leave an account with a lower balance than the rent-exempt minimum) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unbalanced_&#8203;transaction</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The total referenced account lamports before and after the transaction was unbalanced) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bundle_&#8203;peer</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The transaction was part of a bundle and an earlier transaction in the bundle failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;version</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;version="<span class="metrics-enum">legacy</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by transaction version (Legacy transaction format) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;version</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;version="<span class="metrics-enum">v0</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by transaction version (Version 0 transaction format) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;version</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;version="<span class="metrics-enum">v1</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by transaction version (Version 1 transaction format) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;signature_&#8203;checked</span> | counter | Ed25519 signatures checked, including failures |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;poh_&#8203;hash_&#8203;computed</span> | counter | PoH SHA-256 iterations computed |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;instruction_&#8203;executed</span> | counter | Top-level instructions executed |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;cpi_&#8203;executed</span> | counter | Cross-program invocations executed |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;account_&#8203;lthash_&#8203;skipped</span> | counter | Committed writable accounts left byte-identical by their transaction, so their lthash update was skipped |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">load</span>"} | counter | Time per transaction, by phase; a phase counts only once the next phase starts, so dropped, fees-only and no-op transactions lose their trailing phases (Transaction checks and account loading) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">execute</span>"} | counter | Time per transaction, by phase; a phase counts only once the next phase starts, so dropped, fees-only and no-op transactions lose their trailing phases (Transaction execution, including VM setup) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;txn_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">commit</span>"} | counter | Time per transaction, by phase; a phase counts only once the next phase starts, so dropped, fees-only and no-op transactions lose their trailing phases (Transaction result commit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;vm_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{vm_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">setup</span>"} | counter | Time in top-level BPF invocations, by phase, with CPI setup and commit split out; nested programs, including native CPIs, count as Interpreter; subset of TxnRegime Execute (VM setup) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;vm_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{vm_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">commit</span>"} | counter | Time in top-level BPF invocations, by phase, with CPI setup and commit split out; nested programs, including native CPIs, count as Interpreter; subset of TxnRegime Execute (VM commit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;vm_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{vm_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">setup_&#8203;cpi</span>"} | counter | Time in top-level BPF invocations, by phase, with CPI setup and commit split out; nested programs, including native CPIs, count as Interpreter; subset of TxnRegime Execute (VM setup (CPI)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;vm_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{vm_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">commit_&#8203;cpi</span>"} | counter | Time in top-level BPF invocations, by phase, with CPI setup and commit split out; nested programs, including native CPIs, count as Interpreter; subset of TxnRegime Execute (VM commit (CPI)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;vm_&#8203;regime_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span><br/>{vm_&#8203;regime="<span class="metrics-enum">interpreter</span>"} | counter | Time in top-level BPF invocations, by phase, with CPI setup and commit split out; nested programs, including native CPIs, count as Interpreter; subset of TxnRegime Execute (VM interpreter execution) |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;cu_&#8203;consumed</span> | counter | Compute units consumed by executed transactions |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;looked_&#8203;up</span> | counter | Program cache lookups; equals Hit + Miss + lookups of invalid program accounts |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;miss</span> | counter | Program cache misses, each followed by an insert or a spill |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;waited</span> | counter | Program cache lookups that waited on another tile's in-flight load of the same program |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;insert_&#8203;retried</span> | counter | Program cache inserts that found no free slot in their size class on the first try and retried after eviction |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;inserted_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes inserted into the program cache |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;spilled_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes loaded into program cache spill memory |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;evicted_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes evicted from the program cache by insert eviction sweeps |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Time in program cache lookups, including waits on peer loads and insert, eviction and ELF load on a miss; superset of ProgcacheLoadDurationNanos |
| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;loaded</span> | counter | Program ELF loads and verifications for the program cache, including failed verifications and spill-backed loads |

| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;reserve_&#8203;retried</span> | counter | Spin retries while waiting to reserve cache lines for a transaction |

| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read</span> | counter | Reads from the account database file |

| <span class="metrics-name">execrp_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;copied_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Account data bytes copied in memory between cache lines, staging buffers and callers; disk reads excluded |

## Accdb Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Account versions in the index; every unrooted fork version counts |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Account versions the index can hold |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;relocated</span> | counter | Accounts moved in the account database during compaction |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;allocated_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Allocated size of the account database file |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;consumed_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Bytes of the account database file written at least once, excluding never-written partition tail space |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;used_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Bytes of the account database file holding live data |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;is_&#8203;compacting</span> | gauge | Whether a compaction is currently in progress |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;compaction_&#8203;requested</span> | counter | Compactions requested on the account database |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;compaction_&#8203;completed</span> | counter | Compactions fully completed on the account database, less than or equal to the requested amount |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;relocated_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes moved in the account database during compaction |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Dirty accounts written back and evicted by the background preevict pass, by size class; clean lines reclaimed are not counted (0-128 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Dirty accounts written back and evicted by the background preevict pass, by size class; clean lines reclaimed are not counted (129-512 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Dirty accounts written back and evicted by the background preevict pass, by size class; clean lines reclaimed are not counted (513 B-2 KiB) |

| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Dirty accounts written back and evicted by the background preevict pass, by size class; clean lines reclaimed are not counted (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Dirty accounts written back and evicted by the background preevict pass, by size class; clean lines reclaimed are not counted (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Dirty accounts written back and evicted by the background preevict pass, by size class; clean lines reclaimed are not counted (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Dirty accounts written back and evicted by the background preevict pass, by size class; clean lines reclaimed are not counted (1-10 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes read from the account database file by compaction and writeback |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;written_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes written to the account database file by compaction and writeback |
