| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;target</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | gauge | Free cache lines the background preevict pass tries to restore, by size class (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;target</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Free cache lines the background preevict pass tries to restore, by size class (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;target</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Free cache lines the background preevict pass tries to restore, by size class (1-10 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;low_&#8203;water</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;b</span>"} | gauge | Free cache line count below which the background preevict pass starts, by size class (0-128 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;low_&#8203;water</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | gauge | Free cache line count below which the background preevict pass starts, by size class (129-512 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;low_&#8203;water</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | gauge | Free cache line count below which the background preevict pass starts, by size class (513 B-2 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;low_&#8203;water</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le8_&#8203;kib</span>"} | gauge | Free cache line count below which the background preevict pass starts, by size class (2-8 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;low_&#8203;water</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | gauge | Free cache line count below which the background preevict pass starts, by size class (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;low_&#8203;water</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | gauge | Free cache line count below which the background preevict pass starts, by size class (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;low_&#8203;water</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Free cache line count below which the background preevict pass starts, by size class (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class_&#8203;free_&#8203;low_&#8203;water</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | gauge | Free cache line count below which the background preevict pass starts, by size class (1-10 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;preevicted_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes reclaimed from the program cache by the background preevict pass |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Program cache slots reclaimed by the background preevict pass, by size class (0-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Program cache slots reclaimed by the background preevict pass, by size class (128-512 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Program cache slots reclaimed by the background preevict pass, by size class (512 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Program cache slots reclaimed by the background preevict pass, by size class (1-2 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le4_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Program cache slots reclaimed by the background preevict pass, by size class (2-4 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">accdb_&#8203;progcache_&#8203;preevicted</span><br/>{progcache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le11_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Program cache slots reclaimed by the background preevict pass, by size class (4-11 MiB) |

</div>

## Tower Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;pruned_&#8203;fork_&#8203;slot_&#8203;ignored</span> | counter | replay_slot_completed frags we ignored |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;last_&#8203;pruned_&#8203;fork_&#8203;ignored_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Slot of the most recent replay_slot_completed frag ignored for being on a pruned fork, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;last_&#8203;replayed_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Most recently replayed slot, 0 if none; can decrease |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;last_&#8203;vote_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest voted slot in the local tower, 0 if we have not voted |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;reset_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Most recent reset slot; can decrease |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;root_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest rooted slot, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;init_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Init slot, either the snapshot or genesis slot. Set once and does not change |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;frag_&#8203;not_&#8203;ready_&#8203;dropped</span> | counter | Frags dropped before the tower tile initialized (vote txns, gossip updates), plus duplicate-shred messages with no leader schedule |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;eqvoc_&#8203;chunk_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{eqvoc_&#8203;proof_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">verified</span>"} | counter | Equivocation proof chunks and conflicting shreds processed, by result (Completed and verified an equivocation proof) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;eqvoc_&#8203;chunk_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{eqvoc_&#8203;proof_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">ignored</span>"} | counter | Equivocation proof chunks and conflicting shreds processed, by result (Buffered, duplicate, or for a slot already proven) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;eqvoc_&#8203;chunk_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{eqvoc_&#8203;proof_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid</span>"} | counter | Equivocation proof chunks and conflicting shreds processed, by result (Failed validation) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;ghost_&#8203;vote_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{ghost_&#8203;vote_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Votes counted toward ghost, by result (Vote was counted towards ghost) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;ghost_&#8203;vote_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{ghost_&#8203;vote_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">already_&#8203;voted</span>"} | counter | Votes counted toward ghost, by result (Slot was not newer than previous vote) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">pending</span>"} | counter | Votes counted toward hard fork detection, by result (Counted, no 52% agreement yet) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">matched</span>"} | counter | Votes counted toward hard fork detection, by result (Counted, and 52%+ of stake agreed on our bank hash) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">mismatched</span>"} | counter | Votes counted toward hard fork detection, by result (Counted, and 52%+ of stake agreed on a different bank hash (we hard forked)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unknown_&#8203;voter</span>"} | counter | Votes counted toward hard fork detection, by result (Voter not in the voter set) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">already_&#8203;voted</span>"} | counter | Votes counted toward hard fork detection, by result (Voter already voted for this block_id) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;vote_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;old</span>"} | counter | Votes counted toward hard fork detection, by result (Slot not newer than the voter's previous vote) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;bank_&#8203;hash_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;bank_&#8203;hash_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">pending</span>"} | counter | Our own bank hashes checked against hard fork votes, by result (No 52% agreement on this block yet) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;bank_&#8203;hash_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;bank_&#8203;hash_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">matched</span>"} | counter | Our own bank hashes checked against hard fork votes, by result (52%+ of stake agreed on our bank hash) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;bank_&#8203;hash_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;bank_&#8203;hash_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">mismatched</span>"} | counter | Our own bank hashes checked against hard fork votes, by result (52%+ of stake agreed on a different bank hash (we hard forked)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;highest_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;matched_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest slot where 52%+ of stake agreed on our bank hash, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;highest_&#8203;hard_&#8203;fork_&#8203;mismatched_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest slot where 52%+ of stake agreed on a different bank hash than ours (we hard forked), 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;chosen</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">empty_&#8203;tower_&#8203;stale</span>"} | counter | Fork choices for voting and reset, by result (Haven't voted yet and best block is not recent enough to vote (can't vote)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;chosen</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">empty_&#8203;tower_&#8203;vote</span>"} | counter | Fork choices for voting and reset, by result (Haven't voted yet, voting for best block (can vote)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;chosen</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">ancestor_&#8203;rollback</span>"} | counter | Fork choices for voting and reset, by result (Rollback to an ancestor of our prev vote (can't vote)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;chosen</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">sibling_&#8203;confirmed</span>"} | counter | Fork choices for voting and reset, by result (Duplicate sibling got confirmed (can vote)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;chosen</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">same_&#8203;fork</span>"} | counter | Fork choices for voting and reset, by result (Same fork as prev vote (can vote)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;chosen</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">switch_&#8203;passed</span>"} | counter | Fork choices for voting and reset, by result (Prev vote was on a different fork, but we are allowed to switch (can vote)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;chosen</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;fork_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">switch_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Fork choices for voting and reset, by result (Prev vote was on a different fork, and we are not allowed to switch (can't vote)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;gate_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;gate_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">voted</span>"} | counter | Vote gating checks after a fork was chosen, by result (Passed all vote checks and cast a vote) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;gate_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;gate_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">lockout_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Vote gating checks after a fork was chosen, by result (Locked out (can't vote)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;gate_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;gate_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">threshold_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Vote gating checks after a fork was chosen, by result (Did not pass threshold check (can't vote)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;gate_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;gate_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">propagated_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Vote gating checks after a fork was chosen, by result (Prev leader block did not propagate (can't vote)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;gate_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;gate_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;candidate</span>"} | counter | Vote gating checks after a fork was chosen, by result (Fork decision did not produce a votable block) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;tower_&#8203;file_&#8203;written</span> | counter | Times the tower file was written |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;tower_&#8203;file_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Newest vote slot in the last written tower file, 0 if not written yet |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;tower_&#8203;file_&#8203;size_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Size of the last written tower file |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions processed, by result (Vote txn passed per-txn validation and reached slot counting (per-slot outcomes tracked by VoteSlotResult)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">not_&#8203;simple_&#8203;vote</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions processed, by result (Txn was not a simple vote transaction) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bad_&#8203;deser</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions processed, by result (Vote txn failed to deserialize) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">not_&#8203;tower_&#8203;sync</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions processed, by result (Vote txn was not a TowerSync instruction) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">empty_&#8203;tower</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions processed, by result (Vote txn had an empty tower (validator hasn't voted)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bad_&#8203;tower</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions processed, by result (Vote txn deserialized but tower was invalid) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">null_&#8203;block_&#8203;id</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions processed, by result (Last vote had a null block id) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;old</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions processed, by result (Last vote slot was behind the votes root) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">not_&#8203;staked</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions processed, by result (Unable to query stake for vote slot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bad_&#8203;signer</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions processed, by result (Vote txn was not signed by the authorized voter for the vote account at the vote epoch) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Individual vote slots from vote transactions counted, by result (Vote slot was counted) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;new</span>"} | counter | Individual vote slots from vote transactions counted, by result (Vote slot was too far ahead of the votes root) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unknown_&#8203;voter</span>"} | counter | Individual vote slots from vote transactions counted, by result (Vote account was not in the voter set) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">already_&#8203;voted</span>"} | counter | Individual vote slots from vote transactions counted, by result (Voter already voted for this slot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;old</span>"} | counter | Individual vote slots from vote transactions counted, by result (Vote slot was behind the votes root) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;slot_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unknown_&#8203;slot</span>"} | counter | Individual vote slots from vote transactions counted, by result (Vote slot was for a slot we haven't replayed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;intermediate_&#8203;slot_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;intermediate_&#8203;slot_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">counted</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions whose last vote slot was checked before counting intermediate slots, by result (Last vote slot was replayed and matched, intermediate vote slots will be counted) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;intermediate_&#8203;slot_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;intermediate_&#8203;slot_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unreplayed_&#8203;slot</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions whose last vote slot was checked before counting intermediate slots, by result (Last vote slot was not replayed, intermediate vote slots skipped) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;vote_&#8203;intermediate_&#8203;slot_&#8203;checked</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;intermediate_&#8203;slot_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">mismatched_&#8203;block_&#8203;id</span>"} | counter | Vote transactions whose last vote slot was checked before counting intermediate slots, by result (Last vote slot block id did not match, intermediate vote slots skipped) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (0-128 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (129-512 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (513 B-2 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le8_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (2-8 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">tower_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (1-10 MiB) |

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | QUIC packets received |

| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">handshake_&#8203;complete</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Handshake complete, confirming with peer) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">active</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Active connection) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">peer_&#8203;close</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Peer requested close) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">abort</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Connection terminating due to error) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;conn_&#8203;used</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state="<span class="metrics-enum">close_&#8203;pending</span>"} | gauge | Connection slots in use, by state; sum + ConnFree = ConnCapacity (Connection is closing) |

| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;retransmitted</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;encryption_&#8203;level="<span class="metrics-enum">app</span>"} | counter | QUIC packets retransmitted, by encryption level (App data) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;handshake_&#8203;created</span> | counter | Handshake flows created |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;handshake_&#8203;pool_&#8203;full_&#8203;rejected</span> | counter | Handshakes dropped due to alloc fail |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;handshake_&#8203;evicted</span> | counter | Handshakes dropped due to eviction |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;stream_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Stream receive events |

| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">unknown</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (Unknown frame type) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">ack</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (ACK frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">reset_&#8203;stream</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (RESET_STREAM frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">stop_&#8203;sending</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (STOP_SENDING frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">crypto</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (CRYPTO frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">new_&#8203;token</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (NEW_TOKEN frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">stream</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (STREAM frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">max_&#8203;data</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (MAX_DATA frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">max_&#8203;stream_&#8203;data</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (MAX_STREAM_DATA frame) |

| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">data_&#8203;blocked</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (DATA_BLOCKED frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">stream_&#8203;data_&#8203;blocked</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (STREAM_DATA_BLOCKED frame) |

| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">path_&#8203;challenge</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (PATH_CHALLENGE frame) |

| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">ping</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (PING frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">padding</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (PADDING frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{quic_&#8203;frame_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">datagram</span>"} | counter | QUIC frames received, by frame type (DATAGRAM frame) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;frame_&#8203;parse_&#8203;failed</span> | counter | QUIC frames that failed to parse |

| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;meta_&#8203;acquire_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;meta_&#8203;acquire_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">pool_&#8203;empty</span>"} | counter | Attempts to acquire packet metadata for transmission, by result (Packet metadata pool empty) |

| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;ack_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{ack_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">merged</span>"} | counter | Received packets processed for acknowledgement, by result (Merged into existing ACK range) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;ack_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{ack_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">queue_&#8203;full</span>"} | counter | Received packets processed for acknowledgement, by result (Out of buffers) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;ack_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{ack_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">cancelled</span>"} | counter | Received packets processed for acknowledgement, by result (ACK suppressed by handler) |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;service_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of one fd_quic_service call that processed a due connection timer |
| <span class="metrics-name">txsend_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Duration of processing one received packet |

</div>

## Diag Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;bundle_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Bundle subsystem state: 0=disconnected (All bundle tiles disconnected), 1=connecting (At least one bundle tile connecting, none connected or sleeping), 2=connected (At least one bundle tile connected), 3=sleeping (At least one bundle tile sleeping, none connected), 4=disabled (No bundle tiles configured) |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;vote_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Vote subsystem state: 0=disabled (Non-voting or no tower tile), 1=not_started (Tower tile not running or no votes cast yet), 2=delinquent (Vote distance exceeds threshold or vote stalled), 3=voting (Voting normally), 4=inadmissible (Voting, but the vote account fails the validator admission ticket filter) |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;replay_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Replay subsystem state: 0=disabled (No replay tile), 1=not_started (Replay tile not running or slots are zero), 2=behind (Replay lagging behind turbine or reset slot stalled), 3=running (Replay keeping up) |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;turbine_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Turbine subsystem state: 0=disabled (No shred or replay tiles), 1=not_started (Tiles not all running or turbine slot is zero), 2=stalled (Turbine slot not advancing), 3=repair_outpacing (Repair byte throughput exceeds turbine), 4=running (Turbine receiving normally) |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;builder_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | External block builder subsystem state: 0=disabled (No block builder configured), 1=disconnected (Block builder disconnected), 2=connecting (Block builder connection in progress), 3=unhealthy (Connected, but the block builder is not in a usable state), 4=connected (Block builder connected and healthy) |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;system_&#8203;device_&#8203;irq</span> | counter | Device IRQs across all CPUs |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;device_&#8203;irq_&#8203;on_&#8203;tile_&#8203;cpu</span> | counter | Device IRQs on CPUs dedicated to pinned tiles; subset of SystemDeviceIrq |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;system_&#8203;softirq</span><br/>{softirq_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">net</span>"} | counter | Soft-IRQs across all CPUs, by softirq type (NET_TX, NET_RX) |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;system_&#8203;softirq</span><br/>{softirq_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">disk</span>"} | counter | Soft-IRQs across all CPUs, by softirq type (BLOCK) |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;system_&#8203;softirq</span><br/>{softirq_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">other</span>"} | counter | Soft-IRQs across all CPUs, by softirq type (e.g. TIMER, HRTIMER, IRQ_POLL, TASKLET, SCHED, RCU, ...) |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;softirq_&#8203;on_&#8203;tile_&#8203;cpu</span><br/>{softirq_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">net</span>"} | counter | Soft-IRQs on CPUs dedicated to pinned tiles, by softirq type; subset of SystemSoftirq (NET_TX, NET_RX) |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;softirq_&#8203;on_&#8203;tile_&#8203;cpu</span><br/>{softirq_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">disk</span>"} | counter | Soft-IRQs on CPUs dedicated to pinned tiles, by softirq type; subset of SystemSoftirq (BLOCK) |
| <span class="metrics-name">diag_&#8203;softirq_&#8203;on_&#8203;tile_&#8203;cpu</span><br/>{softirq_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">other</span>"} | counter | Soft-IRQs on CPUs dedicated to pinned tiles, by softirq type; subset of SystemSoftirq (e.g. TIMER, HRTIMER, IRQ_POLL, TASKLET, SCHED, RCU, ...) |

</div>

## Event Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;conn_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Telemetry server connection state: 0=disconnected, 1=connecting, 2=authenticating, 3=connected |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;queue_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Events in the queue, sent-but-unacknowledged plus unsent |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;queue_&#8203;unsent_&#8203;count</span> | gauge | Events in the event queue not yet sent to the event service |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;queue_&#8203;evicted</span> | counter | Oldest events overwritten because the queue was full |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;queue_&#8203;used_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Bytes used in the event queue |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;queue_&#8203;capacity_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Event queue capacity |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;event_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | Events written to the stream, including resends after reconnect |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;ack_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Acknowledgements received; one ack may retire many events |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;last_&#8203;acked_&#8203;nonce</span> | gauge | Nonce of the most recently acknowledged event, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;stream_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes sent to the event service |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;stream_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes received from the event service |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;auth_&#8203;failed</span> | counter | Authentication failures with the event service |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;response_&#8203;malformed_&#8203;rejected</span> | counter | Malformed responses received from the event service |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;conn_&#8203;attempted</span> | counter | Connection attempts to the event service |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;handshake_&#8203;timed_&#8203;out</span> | counter | Authentication handshakes that timed out |
| <span class="metrics-name">event_&#8203;credit_&#8203;stalled</span> | counter | Reconnects forced because the event service stopped granting HTTP/2 flow-control credit while data was pending |

</div>

| Metric | Type | Description |

| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (129-512 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (513 B-2 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le8_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (2-8 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;readonly_&#8203;acquired</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Readonly accounts acquired from the account database, by cache size class of the account's current data size (1-10 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (0-128 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le512_&#8203;b</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (129-512 B) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le2_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (513 B-2 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le8_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (2-8 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le32_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (8-32 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le128_&#8203;kib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (32-128 KiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le1_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (128 KiB-1 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;cache_&#8203;missed</span><br/>{accdb_&#8203;cache_&#8203;class="<span class="metrics-enum">le10_&#8203;mib</span>"} | counter | Accounts not found in the account database cache and read from disk, by cache size class (1-10 MiB) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;account_&#8203;waited</span> | counter | Accounts that had to wait for another reader to finish loading them into the cache |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes read from the account database file |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read</span> | counter | Reads from the account database file |
| <span class="metrics-name">rpc_&#8203;accdb_&#8203;copied_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Account data bytes copied in memory between cache lines, staging buffers and callers; disk reads excluded |

## Mlx5 Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Packets successfully published to output links |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes of packets successfully published to output links |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">malformed</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (Undersized, oversized, or malformed Ethernet, IPv4 or UDP headers) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">dst_&#8203;ip_&#8203;mismatch</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (Destination IP does not match the bind address) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">dst_&#8203;port_&#8203;unbound</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (No output link for the destination UDP port) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">gre_&#8203;invalid</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (GRE tunnel peer or headers invalid) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;rx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">gre_&#8203;unconfigured</span>"} | counter | Received packets dropped, by reason (GRE packet with no GRE tunnel configured) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;gre_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | GRE packets decapsulated and published; subset of PktRx |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;rx_&#8203;buffer_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | RX buffers not currently posted to the NIC |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;rx_&#8203;buffer_&#8203;free</span> | gauge | RX buffers currently posted to the NIC |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;completed</span> | counter | Packets completed by the NIC for transmission |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;completed_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes of packets completed by the NIC |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (Invalid packet headers, or GRE packet exceeding the MTU) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;route</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (No matching route) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">unsupported_&#8203;route</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (Unsupported route type) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">interface_&#8203;missing</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (Interface not available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">unsupported_&#8203;interface</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (Interface type not supported) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">source_&#8203;ip_&#8203;missing</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (No source IP address chosen) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;neighbor</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (Next-hop neighbor unresolved) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">ring_&#8203;full</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (TX ring full or no TX buffer available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">gre_&#8203;no_&#8203;route</span>"} | counter | Transmit jobs dropped, by reason (GRE tunnel addresses missing or outer route is itself tunnelled) |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;gre_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx_&#8203;submitted</span> | counter | GRE transmit jobs submitted to the NIC |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;tx_&#8203;buffer_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | TX buffers pending or submitted to the NIC |
| <span class="metrics-name">mlx5_&#8203;tx_&#8203;buffer_&#8203;free</span> | gauge | TX buffers available for a new transmit job |

</div>

## Snapmk Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;job_&#8203;state</span> | gauge | Snapshot creation job state: 0=idle (Idle, waiting for a job), 1=start (Starting), 2=tar_headers (Writing tar headers), 3=manifest (Writing manifest), 4=accdb_cache (Writing cached accounts), 5=accdb_cache_flush (Flushing cached accounts), 6=accdb_cache_finish (Waiting for cached account flush), 7=accdb_disk (Writing on-disk accounts), 8=accdb_disk_flush (Flushing on-disk accounts), 9=accdb_disk_finish (Waiting for on-disk account flush), 10=accdb_delta (Writing incremental accounts), 11=accdb_delta_flush (Flushing incremental accounts), 12=accdb_delta_finish (Waiting for incremental account flush), 13=status_cache (Writing status cache), 14=eof_marker (Writing tar EOF marker), 15=done (Done, notifying replay), 16=fail (Failed, cleaning up), 17=sleep (Sleeping), 18=startup (Waiting for startup), 19=startup_burst (Publishing pre-existing snapshots) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;snapshot_&#8203;started</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | counter | Snapshot creation jobs started, by snapshot type (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;snapshot_&#8203;started</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | counter | Snapshot creation jobs started, by snapshot type (Incremental snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;snapshot_&#8203;finished</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | counter | Snapshot creation jobs finished successfully, by snapshot type (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;snapshot_&#8203;finished</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | counter | Snapshot creation jobs finished successfully, by snapshot type (Incremental snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;last_&#8203;started_&#8203;slot</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | gauge | Slot of the most recently started snapshot creation, by snapshot type, 0 if none since boot (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;last_&#8203;started_&#8203;slot</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | gauge | Slot of the most recently started snapshot creation, by snapshot type, 0 if none since boot (Incremental snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;last_&#8203;finished_&#8203;slot</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">full</span>"} | gauge | Slot of the most recently finished snapshot creation, by snapshot type, 0 if none since boot (Full snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;last_&#8203;finished_&#8203;slot</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | gauge | Slot of the most recently finished snapshot creation, by snapshot type, 0 if none since boot (Incremental snapshot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;compress_&#8203;consumed_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Uncompressed bytes fed to the compressor |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;disk_&#8203;written_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Compressed bytes written to snapshot files |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;cache_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Bytes of the accounts database cache region scanned so far for the snapshot being created. Resets when a snapshot starts and holds at the total once the cached accounts phase finishes. Stays zero for an incremental snapshot, which does not scan the cache |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;cache_&#8203;expected_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Size of the accounts database cache region a cached accounts phase scans |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;incremental_&#8203;account_&#8203;progress</span> | gauge | Accounts changed since the base snapshot that have been written so far for the incremental snapshot being created. Resets when a snapshot starts, and stays zero for a full snapshot, which has no changed account walk |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;incremental_&#8203;account_&#8203;expected</span> | gauge | Accounts changed since the base snapshot, sampled when the incremental snapshot started; 0 for a full snapshot. Replay may record more changes while the job runs, so progress can exceed this |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;io_&#8203;blocked_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Cumulative time blocked on I/O |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;compress_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Cumulative time spent compressing data |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;incremental_&#8203;account_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Distinct accounts changed in rooted slots since the last full snapshot job started; saturates at IncrementalAccountCapacity |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;incremental_&#8203;account_&#8203;capacity</span> | gauge | Maximum changed accounts that can be tracked; once reached, no incremental snapshot can be created until the next full snapshot |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapmk_&#8203;disk_&#8203;allocated_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Logical bytes allocated across managed snapshot files, including partial and retained files |

</div>

## Snapzp Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">snapzp_&#8203;account_&#8203;compressed</span> | counter | Accounts compressed |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapzp_&#8203;compress_&#8203;consumed_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Uncompressed bytes fed to the compressor |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapzp_&#8203;disk_&#8203;written_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Compressed bytes written to snapshot files |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapzp_&#8203;io_&#8203;blocked_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Cumulative time blocked on I/O |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapzp_&#8203;compress_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Cumulative time spent compressing accounts |
| <span class="metrics-name">snapzp_&#8203;cache_&#8203;read_&#8203;torn</span> | counter | Cached account reads torn by eviction, handed back to snapmk to un-claim |

</div>

## Snaprd Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">snaprd_&#8203;disk_&#8203;read_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes read from the account database file |
| <span class="metrics-name">snaprd_&#8203;export_&#8203;progress_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Account database bytes read so far for the current snapshot creation job |
| <span class="metrics-name">snaprd_&#8203;export_&#8203;expected_&#8203;bytes</span> | gauge | Account database bytes to read for the current snapshot creation job |
| <span class="metrics-name">snaprd_&#8203;io_&#8203;blocked_&#8203;duration_&#8203;nanos</span> | counter | Cumulative time blocked on I/O |

</div>

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|

| <span class="metrics-name">snapsv_&#8203;snapshot_&#8203;available</span><br/>{snap_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">incremental</span>"} | gauge | Snapshot files currently available for download (Incremental snapshot) |

## Resolh Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;txn_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">no_&#8203;bank</span>"} | counter | Transactions dropped, by reason (No bank was available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;txn_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">blockhash_&#8203;expired</span>"} | counter | Transactions dropped, by reason (Blockhash expired) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;txn_&#8203;dropped</span><br/>{resolv_&#8203;txn_&#8203;drop_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">bundle_&#8203;peer_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Transactions dropped, by reason (Another transaction in the bundle failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;stash_&#8203;txn_&#8203;inserted</span> | counter | Transactions with an unknown blockhash added to the stash |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;stash_&#8203;txn_&#8203;removed</span><br/>{stash_&#8203;remove_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">published</span>"} | counter | Stashed transactions removed, by reason (Published once its blockhash became known) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;stash_&#8203;txn_&#8203;removed</span><br/>{stash_&#8203;remove_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">evicted</span>"} | counter | Stashed transactions removed, by reason (Evicted as the oldest entry to make room for a new one) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;stash_&#8203;txn_&#8203;removed</span><br/>{stash_&#8203;remove_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">failed</span>"} | counter | Stashed transactions removed, by reason (Removed without publishing because LUT resolution failed or no bank was available) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (The transaction referenced an index in a LUT that didn't exist) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;uninitialized</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (The account referenced as a LUT hasn't been initialized) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;account_&#8203;data</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (The account referenced as a LUT couldn't be parsed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;account_&#8203;owner</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (The account referenced as a LUT wasn't owned by the ALUT program ID) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (The account referenced as a LUT couldn't be found) |
| <span class="metrics-name">resolh_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result (Resolved successfully) |

</div>

## Bank Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result; only the first error per transaction is counted (The transaction referenced an index in a LUT that didn't exist) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;uninitialized</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result; only the first error per transaction is counted (The account referenced as a LUT hasn't been initialized) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;account_&#8203;data</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result; only the first error per transaction is counted (The account referenced as a LUT couldn't be parsed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;account_&#8203;owner</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result; only the first error per transaction is counted (The account referenced as a LUT wasn't owned by the ALUT program ID) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result; only the first error per transaction is counted (The account referenced as a LUT couldn't be found) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;attempted</span><br/>{lut_&#8203;resolve_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Address lookup table resolution attempts, by result; only the first error per transaction is counted (Resolved successfully) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Success) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;in_&#8203;use</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (An account is already being processed in another transaction in a way that does not support parallelism) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;loaded_&#8203;twice</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (A `Pubkey` appears twice in the transaction's `account_keys`. Instructions can reference `Pubkey`s more than once but the message must contain a list with no duplicate keys) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Attempt to debit an account but found no record of a prior credit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">program_&#8203;account_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Attempt to load a program that does not exist) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">insufficient_&#8203;funds_&#8203;for_&#8203;fee</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The fee payer `Pubkey` does not have sufficient balance to pay the fee to schedule the transaction) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;account_&#8203;for_&#8203;fee</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (This account may not be used to pay transaction fees) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">already_&#8203;processed</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The bank has seen this transaction before. This can occur under normal operation when a UDP packet is duplicated, as a user error from a client not updating its `recent_blockhash`, or as a double-spend attack) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">blockhash_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The bank has not seen the given `recent_blockhash` or the transaction is too old and the `recent_blockhash` has been discarded) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">instruction_&#8203;error</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (An error occurred while processing an instruction) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">call_&#8203;chain_&#8203;too_&#8203;deep</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Loader call chain is too deep) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">missing_&#8203;signature_&#8203;for_&#8203;fee</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction requires a fee but has no signature present) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;account_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction contains an invalid account reference) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">signature_&#8203;failure</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction did not pass signature verification) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;program_&#8203;for_&#8203;execution</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (This program may not be used for executing instructions) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">sanitize_&#8203;failure</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction failed to sanitize accounts offsets correctly implies that account locks are not taken for this TX, and should not be unlocked) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">cluster_&#8203;maintenance</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transactions are currently disabled due to cluster maintenance) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">account_&#8203;borrow_&#8203;outstanding</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction processing left an account with an outstanding borrowed reference) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">would_&#8203;exceed_&#8203;max_&#8203;block_&#8203;cost_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction would exceed max Block Cost Limit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unsupported_&#8203;version</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction version is unsupported) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;writable_&#8203;account</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction loads a writable account that cannot be written) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">would_&#8203;exceed_&#8203;max_&#8203;account_&#8203;cost_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction would exceed max account limit within the block) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">would_&#8203;exceed_&#8203;account_&#8203;data_&#8203;block_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction would exceed account data limit within the block) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;many_&#8203;account_&#8203;locks</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction locked too many accounts) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;not_&#8203;found</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Address lookup table not found) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;owner</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Attempted to lookup addresses from an account owned by the wrong program) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;data</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Attempted to lookup addresses from an invalid account) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;address_&#8203;lookup_&#8203;table_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Address table lookup uses an invalid index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;rent_&#8203;paying_&#8203;account</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction leaves an account with a lower balance than rent-exempt minimum) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">would_&#8203;exceed_&#8203;max_&#8203;vote_&#8203;cost_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction would exceed max Vote Cost Limit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">would_&#8203;exceed_&#8203;account_&#8203;data_&#8203;total_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction would exceed total account data limit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">duplicate_&#8203;instruction</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction contains a duplicate instruction that is not allowed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">insufficient_&#8203;funds_&#8203;for_&#8203;rent</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction results in an account with insufficient funds for rent) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">max_&#8203;loaded_&#8203;accounts_&#8203;data_&#8203;size_&#8203;exceeded</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction exceeded max loaded accounts data size cap) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">invalid_&#8203;loaded_&#8203;accounts_&#8203;data_&#8203;size_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (LoadedAccountsDataSizeLimit set for transaction must be greater than 0) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">resanitization_&#8203;needed</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Sanitized transaction differed before/after feature activation. Needs to be resanitized) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">program_&#8203;execution_&#8203;temporarily_&#8203;restricted</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Program execution is temporarily restricted on an account) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unbalanced_&#8203;transaction</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The total balance before the transaction does not equal the total balance after the transaction) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">program_&#8203;cache_&#8203;hit_&#8203;max_&#8203;limit</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The total program cache size hit the maximum allowed limit) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">commit_&#8203;cancelled</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (The process for committing the transaction was cancelled internally) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed</span><br/>{bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bundle_&#8203;peer</span>"} | counter | Transactions loaded and executed, by result (Transaction is part of a bundle and one of the peer transactions failed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;landing</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;landing_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by whether and how they landed in the block (Transaction landed and executed successfully) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;landing</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;landing_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">fees_&#8203;only</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by whether and how they landed in the block (Transaction landed, but was fees only and did not execute) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;landing</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;landing_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">failed</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by whether and how they landed in the block (Transaction landed, but failed to execute) |
| <span class="metrics-name">bank_&#8203;txn_&#8203;processed_&#8203;by_&#8203;landing</span><br/>{txn_&#8203;landing_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">dropped</span>"} | counter | Transactions processed, by whether and how they landed in the block (Transaction did not land) |

</div>

## Pohh Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">pohh_&#8203;begin_&#8203;leader_&#8203;delay_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Delay between when we become leader in a slot and when we receive the bank |
