## Guih Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">guih_&#8203;conn_&#8203;open</span> | gauge | Open HTTP connections to the GUI service, excluding connections upgraded to WebSocket |
| <span class="metrics-name">guih_&#8203;websocket_&#8203;conn_&#8203;open</span> | gauge | Open WebSocket connections to the GUI service |
| <span class="metrics-name">guih_&#8203;websocket_&#8203;frame_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | WebSocket frames sent to all connections to the GUI service |
| <span class="metrics-name">guih_&#8203;websocket_&#8203;frame_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | WebSocket frames received from all connections to the GUI service |
| <span class="metrics-name">guih_&#8203;conn_&#8203;tx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes written to all connections to the GUI service |
| <span class="metrics-name">guih_&#8203;conn_&#8203;rx_&#8203;bytes</span> | counter | Bytes read from all connections to the GUI service |

</div>

## Rotor Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;pkt_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | Network packets sent, including requests, pings and pongs |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">pong</span>"} | counter | Signed repair messages queued, by type; window types send one per missing shred (up to 32), others one per attempt (Pong) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">window_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Signed repair messages queued, by type; window types send one per missing shred (up to 32), others one per attempt (Shred at an index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">highest_&#8203;window_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Signed repair messages queued, by type; window types send one per missing shred (up to 32), others one per attempt (Highest shred at or above an index) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">orphan</span>"} | counter | Signed repair messages queued, by type; window types send one per missing shred (up to 32), others one per attempt (Ancestors of an orphan slot) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">parent_&#8203;fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;count</span>"} | counter | Signed repair messages queued, by type; window types send one per missing shred (up to 32), others one per attempt (Parent and FEC set count of a block (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;root</span>"} | counter | Signed repair messages queued, by type; window types send one per missing shred (up to 32), others one per attempt (Merkle root of a FEC set (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;tx</span><br/>{repair_&#8203;request_&#8203;type="<span class="metrics-enum">window_&#8203;index_&#8203;for_&#8203;block_&#8203;id</span>"} | counter | Signed repair messages queued, by type; window types send one per missing shred (up to 32), others one per attempt (Shred at an index of a specific block (Alpenglow)) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;highest_&#8203;delivered_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest slot fully delivered to replay, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;highest_&#8203;received_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest slot a shred was received for, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;first_&#8203;turbine_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | First turbine slot seen, the catchup target, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;catchup_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest slot past the window with a verified cert, the staked catchup target, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;block_&#8203;pending</span> | gauge | Blocks in the eager, notar and final block treaps, waiting for discovery |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;in_&#8203;flight</span> | gauge | Pending request attempts awaiting a first response; a hedge is another attempt |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;fec_&#8203;delivered</span> | counter | FEC sets published to replay |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;shred_&#8203;old_&#8203;ignored</span> | counter | Shreds received at or below the root, ignored |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;shred_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;response_&#8203;match_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">for_&#8203;block_&#8203;id</span>"} | counter | Data shreds received as repair responses, by the pending request they matched (First response to a WindowIndexForBlockId request) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;shred_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;response_&#8203;match_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">slot_&#8203;index</span>"} | counter | Data shreds received as repair responses, by the pending request they matched (First response to a WindowIndex or HighestWindowIndex request) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;shred_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{shred_&#8203;response_&#8203;match_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unmatched</span>"} | counter | Data shreds received as repair responses, by the pending request they matched (Matched no pending request, including later answers to an already matched or expired request) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;meta_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Alpenglow repair metadata responses received |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;meta_&#8203;malformed_&#8203;rejected</span> | counter | Metadata responses that failed to decode |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;meta_&#8203;unsolicited_&#8203;rejected</span> | counter | Metadata responses matching no outstanding request |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;parent_&#8203;fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;count_&#8203;accepted</span> | counter | ParentFecSetCount responses accepted |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;parent_&#8203;fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;count_&#8203;failed</span> | counter | ParentFecSetCount responses that failed verification |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;root_&#8203;accepted</span> | counter | FecSetRoot responses accepted |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;fec_&#8203;set_&#8203;root_&#8203;failed</span> | counter | FecSetRoot responses that failed verification |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;replay_&#8203;root_&#8203;advanced_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Root advanced messages received from replay |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;replay_&#8203;missing_&#8203;fec_&#8203;rx</span> | counter | Missing FEC messages received from replay, each a from-root reconsume |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;ping_&#8203;rejected</span><br/>{ping_&#8203;reject_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">malformed</span>"} | counter | Pings rejected, by reason (Failed to decode) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;ping_&#8203;rejected</span><br/>{ping_&#8203;reject_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">unknown_&#8203;peer</span>"} | counter | Pings rejected, by reason (Sender is not a known peer) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;ping_&#8203;rejected</span><br/>{ping_&#8203;reject_&#8203;reason="<span class="metrics-enum">signature_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Pings rejected, by reason (Signature did not verify) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;response_&#8203;latency_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Time from sending a repair request to receiving its response |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;retry_&#8203;delay_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Delay scheduled before a request's next attempt: its peer's hedge time, 16.8 ms to 100 ms, 16.8 ms after an escape |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;timeout_&#8203;pending</span> | gauge | Timeouts in the eager, notar and final timeout queues |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;timeout_&#8203;cancelled</span> | counter | Timeouts dropped because their block is gone or their work is done |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;timeout_&#8203;no_&#8203;peer_&#8203;deferred</span> | counter | Timeouts deferred because no peer could be picked |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;hedged</span> | counter | Request attempts after the first for the same timer |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;expired</span> | counter | Pending requests with no response after about 1 s (PENDING_TTL); a peer is banned for 10 s after 32 in a row, pending-pool evictions not counted |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;by_&#8203;id_&#8203;expired</span> | counter | WindowIndexForBlockId requests that expired unanswered, at or above the root |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;stuck_&#8203;escaped</span> | counter | Retries sent to a random staked peer because 3 attempts in a row brought no new shred of the FEC set |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;request_&#8203;late_&#8203;retried</span> | counter | Request attempts sent after 3 or more earlier attempts for the same timer |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;escape_&#8203;peer_&#8203;hedge_&#8203;delay_&#8203;nanos</span> | histogram | Uncapped hedge time (srtt+4*rttvar, 100 ms if unmeasured) of each staked peer an escaped retry was sent to |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;peer_&#8203;by_&#8203;id_&#8203;banned</span> | counter | Peers banned for 10 s after 4 WindowIndexForBlockId requests in a row expired, whatever their other replies |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;ping_&#8203;probe_&#8203;tx</span> | counter | Requests sent to elicit a ping from a peer with a new address |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;eager_&#8203;delay_&#8203;nanos</span> | gauge | Cluster-wide wait for turbine before a missing FEC set is repaired (srtt+4*rttvar, clamped to 20-250 ms; 250 ms until sampled) |
| <span class="metrics-name">rotor_&#8203;sign_&#8203;request_&#8203;pending</span> | gauge | Messages in the sign pool waiting for a signature from the sign tiles |

</div>

## Votor Tile

<div class="metrics">

| Metric | Type | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;datagram_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{datagram_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">vote</span>"} | counter | Inbound datagrams dispatched, by result (Datagram carried a vote and was handed to vote rx) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;datagram_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{datagram_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">cert</span>"} | counter | Inbound datagrams dispatched, by result (Datagram carried a cert and was handed to cert rx) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;datagram_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{datagram_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">not_&#8203;ready</span>"} | counter | Inbound datagrams dispatched, by result (Datagram arrived before the votor tile was initialized) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;datagram_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{datagram_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">too_&#8203;small</span>"} | counter | Inbound datagrams dispatched, by result (Datagram was too small to hold a version and a tag) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;datagram_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{datagram_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unknown_&#8203;tag</span>"} | counter | Inbound datagrams dispatched, by result (Datagram tag was neither a vote nor a cert) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;datagram_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{datagram_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">not_&#8203;admitted</span>"} | counter | Inbound datagrams dispatched, by result (Datagram arrived on a connection not yet admitted, or already closed) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Vote was handed to the pool and accepted) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bad_&#8203;size</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Vote was truncated or had trailing bytes) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bad_&#8203;encoding</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Vote had an invalid version, tag, or signature encoding) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unknown_&#8203;signer</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Sending connection had no authenticated identity) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">not_&#8203;peer</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Sender was not in the peer set) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">banned</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Sender is banned for a failed signature verification) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">slot_&#8203;out_&#8203;of_&#8203;range</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Vote slot was either too old or too far in the future) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">null_&#8203;block_&#8203;hash</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Vote block hash was null) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">signature_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Vote signature was the identity point) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unranked</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Sender was not a ranked validator in the vote slot's epoch) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">shred_&#8203;version_&#8203;mismatched</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Vote was for a different shred version) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">duplicate</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Voter had already cast this vote) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{vote_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">slashable</span>"} | counter | Inbound votes processed, by result (Vote constitutes a slashable offence) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">success</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Cert was handed to the pool and accepted) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bad_&#8203;size</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Cert was truncated or had trailing bytes) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">bad_&#8203;encoding</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Cert had an invalid version, tag, bitmap, or signature encoding) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">shred_&#8203;version_&#8203;mismatched</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Cert was for a different shred version) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unknown_&#8203;signer</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Sending connection had no authenticated identity) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">not_&#8203;peer</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Sender was not in the peer set) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">unranked</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Sender was not a ranked validator in the cert slot's epoch) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">slot_&#8203;out_&#8203;of_&#8203;range</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Cert slot was either too old or too far in the future) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">duplicate</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Cert was already in the pool) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">signature_&#8203;failed</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Cert failed the stake threshold or the aggregate signature check) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;rx</span><br/>{cert_&#8203;rx_&#8203;result="<span class="metrics-enum">banned</span>"} | counter | Inbound certificates processed, by result (Sender is banned for a failed signature verification) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;bls_&#8203;signature_&#8203;checked</span> | counter | Aggregate BLS signature checks of votes, certificates and reward certificates; a search for bad signers counts once |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;leader_&#8203;window_&#8203;superseded</span> | counter | Leader windows given up because certificates already let a later window start, eg. while catching up after a restart |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;cert_&#8203;tx_&#8203;unstaked_&#8203;skipped</span> | counter | Certificates and standstill votes not sent because we are not staked in their epoch |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;voting_&#8203;slot_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Slots tracked by the voting state machine |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;voting_&#8203;slot_&#8203;free</span> | gauge | Free entries in the voting state machine's slot table |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;highest_&#8203;final_&#8203;cert_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest slot with a final or fast-final cert seen by votor, 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;pool_&#8203;slot_&#8203;used</span> | gauge | Slots tracked by the vote and cert pool |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;pool_&#8203;slot_&#8203;free</span> | gauge | Free entries in the vote and cert pool's slot table |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;highest_&#8203;pool_&#8203;finalized_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | Highest slot the pool has finalized (slow-final also needs the slot notarized), 0 if none |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;rank</span> | gauge | Rank of this validator in the current epoch (0 is the highest rank), ULONG_MAX if unranked (unstaked) |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;peer_&#8203;conn_&#8203;open</span> | gauge | Peers with an open outbound connection |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;vote_&#8203;history_&#8203;file_&#8203;written</span> | counter | Times the vote history file was written |
| <span class="metrics-name">votor_&#8203;wait_&#8203;to_&#8203;vote_&#8203;slot</span> | gauge | First slot votor may vote in, past the vote history file and the votes it sent |

</div>
